0001  @inherits VmComponent
0002  <div class="notice">@Software.Limitation 正值表示目标较低，负值表示目标较高；不能据此保证因果或路径独立性。</div>
0003  <section class="panel">
0004      <h2>显示筛选与频带扫描</h2>
0005      <div class="filter-grid">
0006          <label class="field"><span>用途</span><select name="analysis-purpose" @bind="Vm.Filter.Purpose"><option value="">全部</option>@foreach (var purpose in Enum.GetValues<PairPurpose>()) { <option value="@purpose">@purpose.Text()</option> }</select></label>
0007          <label class="field"><span>来源</span><select @bind="Vm.Filter.Source"><option value="">全部</option>@foreach (var source in Enum.GetValues<SourceKind>()) { <option value="@source">@source.Text()</option> }</select></label>
0008          <label class="field"><span>配对状态</span><select @bind="Vm.Filter.Status"><option value="">全部</option><option value="@ResultStatus.Valid">可比较</option><option value="@ResultStatus.Blocked">已阻断</option><option value="@ResultStatus.Empty">无有效数据</option></select></label>
0009          <label class="field"><span>目标位置</span><select @bind="Vm.Filter.Location"><option value="">全部</option>@foreach (var location in Vm.Project.Locations) { <option value="@location.Id">@location.Name</option> }</select></label>
0010          <label class="field"><span>名称查找</span><input name="analysis-search" @bind="Vm.Filter.Search" /></label>
0011          <label class="field"><span>频率下限 Hz</span><input name="scan-min" inputmode="decimal" @bind="Vm.Filter.MinimumFrequency" /></label>
0012          <label class="field"><span>频率上限 Hz</span><input name="scan-max" inputmode="decimal" @bind="Vm.Filter.MaximumFrequency" /></label>
0013      </div>
0014      <div class="toolbar"><button class="primary" data-testid="apply-scan" @onclick="Vm.ApplyScan">应用显示筛选</button><button @onclick="Vm.Filter.Reset">恢复全部</button><small>@Vm.Filter.AppliedDescription；显示筛选不改变原始数据和导出报告。</small></div>
0015  </section>
0016  @if (Vm.Filter.Pairs(Vm.Analysis).Count == 0)
0017  {
0018      <div class="empty">暂无符合条件的配对。先在“显式试验配对”建立对应关系，或恢复全部筛选。</div>
0019  }
0020  @foreach (var pair in Vm.Filter.Pairs(Vm.Analysis))
0021  {
0022      <section class="panel result-card" data-result-pair="@pair.PairId">
0023          <div class="section-heading">
0024              <div><p class="eyebrow">@pair.Purpose.Text() · @pair.Source.Text()</p><h2>@pair.Title</h2></div>
0025              <StatusBadge Status="pair.Status" />
0026          </div>
0027          @if (pair.BlockingReasons.Count > 0)
0028          {
0029              <div class="error-list"><strong>阻断原因</strong><ul>@foreach (var reason in pair.BlockingReasons) { <li>@reason</li> }</ul></div>
0030          }
0031          @if (pair.Warnings.Count > 0)
0032          {
0033              <p class="muted">@string.Join("；", pair.Warnings)</p>
0034          }
0035          <PairChart Pair="Vm.Filter.ChartPair(pair)" />
0036          <div class="table-wrap">
0037              <table data-testid="pair-bands">
0038                  <thead><tr><th>Hz</th><th>基线 dB</th><th>目标 dB</th><th>基线−目标 dB</th><th>状态与解释</th></tr></thead>
0039                  <tbody>
0040                      @foreach (var band in Vm.Filter.Bands(pair))
0041                      {
0042                          <tr>
0043                              <td>@band.Frequency</td><td>@Software.Format(band.Baseline)</td><td>@Software.Format(band.Target)</td>
0044                              <td><strong>@Software.Format(band.Difference)</strong></td><td><StatusBadge Status="band.Status" /><small class="block">@band.Interpretation</small></td>
0045                          </tr>
0046                      }
0047                  </tbody>
0048              </table>
0049          </div>
0050          <div class="result-note"><strong>录入频带合成差 @Software.Format(pair.TotalDifference) dB</strong><span>@pair.TotalNote</span></div>
0051          @if (pair.Screening is { } screening)
0052          {
0053              <div class="screening"><StatusBadge Status="screening.Status" />最大频带绝对差 @Software.Format(screening.MaximumAbsoluteDifference) dB；最差频率 @Software.Format(screening.WorstFrequency) Hz；阈值 @Software.Format(screening.Threshold) dB。@Software.ScreeningNote</div>
0054          }
0055          <details>
0056              <summary>来源、顺序及配对身份</summary>
0057              <p>基线来源：@pair.BaselineSource</p><p>目标来源：@pair.TargetSource</p>
0058              <p>目标减基线时间 @pair.ElapsedSeconds s；配对 <code>@pair.PairId</code></p>
0059          </details>
0060      </section>
0061  }
0062  <section class="panel">
0063      <h2>单项与组合测得差值矩阵</h2>
0064      <p class="muted">每列使用各自的显式配对。条件与来源可能不同；这里只并列展示测得差值，不作独立贡献排序，不生成“单项相加预测”列。</p>
0065      @{ var matrixPairs = Vm.Filter.Pairs(Vm.Analysis).Where(pair => pair.Purpose == PairPurpose.Effect).ToList(); }
0066      <div class="table-wrap">
0067          <table data-testid="matrix">
0068              <thead>
0069                  <tr><th>频率 Hz</th>@foreach (var pair in matrixPairs) { <th>@pair.TargetName<small class="block">基线：@pair.BaselineName · @pair.Source.Text()</small></th> }</tr>
0070              </thead>
0071              <tbody>
0072                  @foreach (var frequency in Vm.Filter.MatrixFrequencies(matrixPairs))
0073                  {
0074                      <tr>
0075                          <td>@frequency</td>
0076                          @foreach (var pair in matrixPairs)
0077                          {
0078                              var band = pair.Bands.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Frequency == frequency);
0079                              <td>@if (band?.Difference is { } difference) { <strong>@Software.Format(difference)</strong> } else if (band is not null) { <StatusBadge Status="band.Status" /> } else { <span>未声明</span> }</td>
0080                          }
0081                      </tr>
0082                  }
0083              </tbody>
0084          </table>
0085      </div>
0086  </section>
0087  <section class="panel">
0088      <h2>同方案重复配对统计</h2>
0089      <p class="muted">按位置集合和来源分组，所有配对之间再检查工况。配对差的算术均值与组能量平均声级之差是两个指标，不能混同。区间未计仪器系统不确定性。</p>
0090      @foreach (var effect in Vm.Analysis.Effects)
0091      {
0092          <h3>@Vm.Project.LocationNames(effect.LocationIds) · @effect.Source.Text()</h3>
0093          @if (effect.BlockingReasons.Count > 0)
0094          {
0095              <div class="error-list">@string.Join("；", effect.BlockingReasons)</div>
0096          }
0097          <div class="table-wrap">
0098              <table>
0099                  <thead><tr><th>Hz</th><th>n</th><th>配对差均值 dB</th><th>差值SD dB</th><th>组能量均值差 dB</th><th>双侧95%区间 dB</th></tr></thead>
0100                  <tbody>
0101                      @foreach (var band in effect.Bands.Where(band => Vm.Filter.IncludesFrequency(band.Frequency)))
0102                      {
0103                          <tr>
0104                              <td>@band.Frequency</td><td>@band.Statistics.Count</td><td>@Software.Format(band.Statistics.MeanDifference)</td>
0105                              <td>@Software.Format(band.Statistics.StandardDeviation)</td><td>@Software.Format(band.Statistics.GroupEnergyMeanDifference)</td>
0106                              <td>@(band.Statistics.Lower95.HasValue ? Software.Format(band.Statistics.Lower95) + " 至 " + Software.Format(band.Statistics.Upper95) : "不生成")</td>
0107                          </tr>
0108                      }
0109                  </tbody>
0110              </table>
0111          </div>
0112          <p class="muted">@(effect.Bands.FirstOrDefault()?.Statistics.IntervalNote)</p>
0113      }
0114  </section>

0001  @inherits VmComponent
0002  <div class="editor-layout">
0003      <aside class="object-list">
0004          <div class="section-heading"><h2>工况列表</h2><span>@Vm.Project.Conditions.Count</span></div>

0006          @foreach (var item in Vm.Project.Conditions)
0007          {
0008              <button class="object @(Vm.Condition.Id == item.Id ? "active" : "")"
0009                      data-condition="@item.Id" @onclick="() => Vm.SelectCondition(item.Id)">
0010                  <strong>@item.Name</strong><small>@item.Vehicle · @item.Weighting</small>
0011              </button>
0012          }
0013      </aside>
0014      <section class="panel">
0015          <div class="section-heading"><h2>@(Vm.Condition.IsExisting ? "编辑" : "新增")工况</h2><code>@Vm.Condition.IdentityLabel</code></div>
0016          <div class="notice">允许保存工况草稿；必要字段缺失时阻断比较。修改已引用工况会重新计算全部相关结果。</div>
0017          <form @onsubmit="Vm.SaveCondition" @onsubmit:preventDefault novalidate>
0018              <fieldset class="form-grid" disabled="@Vm.Archived">
0019                  <EditorField Label="工况名称 *" Name="condition-name" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Name" Wide MaximumLength="120" />
0020                  <EditorField Label="车辆标识" Name="condition-vehicle" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Vehicle" MaximumLength="500" />
0021                  <EditorField Label="测点及安装位置" Name="condition-microphone" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Microphone" MaximumLength="500" />
0022                  <EditorField Label="声源 / 激励方式" Name="condition-excitation" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Excitation" MaximumLength="500" />
0023                  <EditorField Label="路面 / 试验场" Name="condition-surface" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Surface" MaximumLength="500" />
0024                  <EditorField Label="挡位" Name="condition-gear" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Gear" MaximumLength="500" />
0025                  <EditorField Label="车速 km/h" Name="condition-speed" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Speed" InputMode="decimal" Help="0—500；静态可填0，未知留空。" />
0026                  <EditorField Label="转速 r/min" Name="condition-rpm" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Rpm" InputMode="decimal" Help="0—30000；未知不补零。" />
0027                  <EditorField Label="温度 °C" Name="condition-temperature" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Temperature" InputMode="decimal" Help="-80—100，按实际记录填写。" />
0028                  <EditorField Label="积分时长 s" Name="condition-duration" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Duration" InputMode="decimal" Help="0.001—86400；须与统计定义对应。" />
0029                  <label class="field"><span>频率计权</span><select name="condition-weighting" @bind="Vm.Condition.Weighting" @bind:after="Vm.MarkEditorDirty"><option value="">未填写</option><option>A</option><option>C</option><option>Z</option></select><small>输入须已采用该计权，软件不重复加权。</small></label>
0030                  <EditorField Label="时间计权 / 积分定义" Name="condition-time" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.TimeDefinition" MaximumLength="500" Help="例如Leq，必须与仪器统计口径一致。" />
0031                  <label class="field"><span>频带制</span><select name="condition-band-type" @bind="Vm.Condition.BandType" @bind:after="Vm.MarkEditorDirty"><option value="">未填写</option><option value="octave">倍频程</option><option value="third-octave">三分之一倍频程</option><option value="custom">自定义</option></select></label>
0032                  <EditorField Label="仪器标识" Name="condition-instrument" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Instrument" MaximumLength="500" />
0033                  <EditorField Label="标定链路及状态" Name="condition-calibration" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Calibration" MaximumLength="500" Help="按真实标定记录填写；软件不认证标定有效性。" />
0034                  <label class="field"><span>声级单位及参考</span><select name="condition-unit" @bind="Vm.Condition.Unit" @bind:after="Vm.MarkEditorDirty"><option value="">未填写</option><option>@Software.LevelUnit</option></select></label>
0035                  <EditorField Label="采样率 Hz（条件规则可选检查）" Name="condition-sample-rate" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.SampleRate" InputMode="decimal" />
0036                  <EditorField Label="时钟与同步定义（条件规则可选检查）" Name="condition-sync" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Synchronization" MaximumLength="500" />
0037                  <label class="check wide"><input name="condition-nonoverlap" type="checkbox" @bind="Vm.Condition.NonOverlappingBands" @bind:after="Vm.MarkEditorDirty" />确认输入频带互不重叠，允许计算录入频带范围的合成声级</label>
0038                  <EditorField Label="工况备注" Name="condition-notes" @bind-Value="Vm.Condition.Notes" Area Wide />
0039                  <EditorActions Existing="Vm.Condition.IsExisting" New="() => Vm.SelectCondition(null)" Delete='() => Vm.DeleteSelected("condition", Vm.Condition.Id)' />
0040              </fieldset>
0041          </form>
0042      </section>
0043  </div>

0001  @inherits VmComponent
0002  <section class="panel">
0003      <div class="section-heading"><div><h2>保存当前工作副本到本机</h2><p>先应用表单，再显式保存。软件不会在关闭页面时自动落盘。</p></div><button class="primary" @onclick="Vm.SaveAsync">保存项目到本机目录</button></div>
0004      <dl class="details">
0005          <dt>项目目录</dt><dd class="path">@Vm.StoreDirectory</dd>
0006          <dt>当前文件</dt><dd class="path">@(string.IsNullOrEmpty(Vm.Session.SavedPath) ? "尚未保存" : Vm.Session.SavedPath)</dd>
0007          <dt>最近保存位置</dt><dd class="path">@Vm.LastSavedPath</dd>
0008          <dt>上次版本备份</dt><dd class="path">@(string.IsNullOrEmpty(Vm.LastBackupPath) ? "首次保存没有旧文件备份" : Vm.LastBackupPath)</dd>
0009          <dt>保存方式</dt><dd>同目录临时文件写入并刷新后替换；覆盖前保留.bak。外部修改与ETag不符时阻止覆盖。</dd>
0010      </dl>
0011      <div class="notice">冲突时不要反复覆盖。可先“复制为新项目”保存独立文件，或回项目库重新打开磁盘版后编辑。备份打开会标为未保存，复核后再显式保存。</div>
0012  </section>
0013  <div class="export-grid">
0014      <section class="panel export-card">
0015          <p class="eyebrow">SEALPROJ / 项目</p><h2>搬迁与归档文件</h2>
0016          <p>下载内嵌全部对象、频带、来源、配对、参数、审计和复核的.sealproj。下载不替代本机保存。</p>
0017          <button @onclick='() => Vm.PrepareExport("project")'>准备项目下载</button>
0018      </section>
0019      <section class="panel export-card">
0020          <p class="eyebrow">HTML / 报告</p><h2>自包含分析报告</h2>
0021          <p>原始数据、工况、图表、测得差值、重复统计、漂移恢复、覆盖、来源、问题与审计。浏览器可打印PDF。</p>
0022          <button data-testid="prepare-report" @onclick='() => Vm.PrepareExport("report")'>准备HTML分析报告</button>
0023      </section>
0024      <section class="panel export-card">
0025          <p class="eyebrow">CSV / 结果</p><h2>可追溯频带结果</h2>
0026          <p>逐配对逐频带差值、状态、原因、来源、单位、项目/工况标识、软件版本、参数JSON和输入哈希。</p>
0027          <button data-testid="prepare-results" @onclick='() => Vm.PrepareExport("results")'>准备结果CSV</button>
0028      </section>
0029      <section class="panel export-card">
0030          <p class="eyebrow">CSV / 交换</p><h2>原始频带长表</h2>
0031          <p>trial_id、frequency_hz、level_db三列，缺失为空，UTF-8。仅交换频带，不含全部项目元数据。</p>
0032          <button @onclick='() => Vm.PrepareExport("measurements")'>准备原始频带CSV</button>
0033      </section>
0034      <section class="panel export-card">
0035          <p class="eyebrow">JSON / 分析快照</p><h2>机器可检查的完整结果</h2>
0036          <p>包含项目输入、所有计算输出、版本、时间、单位、限制和输入哈希，便于独立核对；该文件不作为项目文件重新打开。</p>
0037          <button @onclick='() => Vm.PrepareExport("analysis")'>准备分析JSON</button>
0038      </section>
0039  </div>
0040  @if (Vm.DownloadUrl.Length > 0)
0041  {
0042      <section class="panel download-ready">
0043          <h2>下载已准备</h2>
0044          <a class="button primary" data-testid="download-export" href="@Vm.DownloadUrl" download="@Vm.DownloadFilename">下载 @Vm.DownloadFilename</a>
0045          <p>点击链接后在浏览器下载记录确认实际路径。链接保留约10分钟；过期请重新准备。</p>
0046      </section>
0047  }
0048  <section class="panel">
0049      <h2>保存、搬迁、重开核对</h2>
0050      <ol class="steps">
0051          <li>应用当前表单，点击“保存项目”。查看成功消息和实际路径。</li>
0052          <li>回“项目工作台”，刷新本机项目库，选择刚保存的项目打开，核对修订、试验数和配对结果。</li>
0053          <li>准备并下载.sealproj，把文件复制到另一目录。通过项目页“导入已有文件”重新打开，确认不依赖原CSV路径。</li>
0054          <li>导入的同标识项目不会直接覆盖本机现有文件。要保存到新文件，先复制为新项目；要续改原文件，先从项目库打开。</li>
0055          <li>导出HTML报告与结果CSV，核对来源、Hz、dB参考、参数、版本和阻断原因。报告与项目修订应一致。</li>
0056      </ol>
0057  </section>
0058  <section class="panel">
0059      <h2>使用边界</h2>
0060      <p>@Software.Limitation</p>
0061      <p>本版本处理已经提取且完成所声明计权的频带声级。没有音频频谱、声源分离、自动标定、标准认证、账号权限或远程协作功能。来源真实性由操作者按原始记录核实。</p>
0062      <p>软件仅监听本机。本机保存目录与下载文件由用户按数据权限、备份和留存要求管理；不在报告中填写未知的申请人、权属或开发发表日期。</p>
0063  </section>

src/AcousticSeal.App/Components/Pages/HistoryPage.razor
================================================================================
0001  @inherits VmComponent
0002  <section class="panel" @oninput="Vm.MarkReviewDirty">
0003      <h2>记录当前分析复核快照</h2>
0004      <p>输入复核人及本次判断。记录保存当前输入修订、参数、有效与阻断配对数量、全部阻断原因和SHA-256。哈希用于内容核对，不是签名；本地项目可被用户编辑。</p>
0005      <div class="form-grid">
0006          <EditorField Label="复核人 *（实际填写）" Name="reviewer" @bind-Value="Vm.Reviewer" TrackDirty="false" MaximumLength="120" />
0007          <div class="help-box">当前输入修订 @Vm.Project.Revision；有效 @Vm.Analysis.ValidPairCount，阻断 @Vm.Analysis.BlockedPairCount。</div>
0008          <EditorField Label="复核意见与后续补测安排" Name="review-comment" @bind-Value="Vm.ReviewComment" TrackDirty="false" Area Wide />
0009          <div class="wide"><button class="primary" data-testid="record-review" @onclick="Vm.RecordReview" disabled="@Vm.Archived">记录当前分析复核</button></div>
0010      </div>
0011  </section>
0012  <section class="panel">
0013      <h2>复核记录</h2>
0014      @if (Vm.Project.Reviews.Count == 0)
0015      {
0016          <div class="empty">还没有复核记录。复核只代表用户记录的判断，不代表软件已认证测量有效。</div>
0017      }
0018      @foreach (var review in Vm.Project.Reviews.AsEnumerable().Reverse())
0019      {
0020          <details class="review-card">
0021              <summary>@Software.Time(review.Time) · @review.Reviewer · 输入修订 @review.InputRevision</summary>
0022              <p>@review.Comment</p>
0023              <p>有效 @review.ValidPairs；阻断 @review.BlockedPairs。输入 <code>@review.InputHash</code></p>
0024              <h3>复核当时的阻断原因</h3>
0025              <ul>@foreach (var reason in review.BlockingReasons) { <li>@reason</li> }</ul>
0026              <h3>复核当时的参数</h3>
0027              <pre>@review.SettingsJson</pre>
0028          </details>
0029      }
0030  </section>

0102  @code {
0103      private async Task ReadCsvAsync(InputFileChangeEventArgs args)
0104      {
0105          try
0106          {
0107              var bytes = await UploadBoundary.ReadAsync(args.File, 5 * 1024 * 1024);
0108              Vm.LoadCsv(bytes, args.File.Name);
0109          }
0110          catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or DomainValidationException or InvalidOperationException)
0111          {
0112              Vm.Edit(() => throw new DomainValidationException("csv.file", error.Message));
0113          }
0114      }
0115  }

0100  @code {
0101      private static bool Eligible(TrialKind kind, PairPurpose purpose)
0102      {
0103          return purpose switch
0104          {
0105              PairPurpose.Effect => kind is TrialKind.Single or TrialKind.Combination,
0106              PairPurpose.Drift => kind == TrialKind.Baseline,
0107              PairPurpose.Recovery => kind == TrialKind.Recovery,
0108              _ => false
0109          };
0110      }
0111  }

0076  @code {
0077      private async Task UploadAsync(InputFileChangeEventArgs args)
0078      {
0079          try
0080          {
0081              var file = args.File;
0082              var bytes = await UploadBoundary.ReadAsync(file, Software.MaximumFileBytes);
0083              Vm.ImportProject(bytes, file.Name);
0084          }
0085          catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or DomainValidationException or InvalidOperationException)
0086          {
0087              Vm.Edit(() => throw new DomainValidationException("file", error.Message));
0088          }
0089      }

0067      </section>
0068  }
0069
0070  @code {
0071      private async Task LoadAsync(InputFileChangeEventArgs args, bool isLeft)
0072      {
0073          try
0074          {
0075              var bytes = await UploadBoundary.ReadAsync(args.File, Software.MaximumFileBytes);
0076              Vm.Edit(() => Vm.Revision.Load(isLeft, bytes, args.File.Name));
0077          }
0078          catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or DomainValidationException or InvalidOperationException)
0079          {
0080              Vm.Edit(() => throw new DomainValidationException("revision.file", error.Message));
0081          }
0082      }
0083
0084      private static string Preview(string text)
0085      {
0086          return text.Length > 100 ? text[..100] + "…展开完整内容" : text.Length == 0 ? "空文本" : text;
0087      }
0088  }

src/AcousticSeal.App/Components/Shared/EditorField.razor
================================================================================
0001  @inject WorkspaceViewModel Workspace
0002  <label class="field @(Wide ? "wide" : "")">
0003      <span>@Label</span>
0004      @if (Area)
0005      {
0006          <textarea name="@Name" rows="@Rows" maxlength="@MaximumLength"
0007                    value="@Value" @oninput="ChangeAsync" spellcheck="false"></textarea>
0008      }
0009      else
0010      {
0011          <input name="@Name" type="text" value="@Value" maxlength="@MaximumLength"
0012                 inputmode="@InputMode" @oninput="ChangeAsync" autocomplete="off" />
0013      }
0014      @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Help))
0015      {
0016          <small>@Help</small>
0017      }
0018  </label>
0019
0020  @code {
0021      [Parameter] public string Label { get; set; } = "";
0022      [Parameter] public string Name { get; set; } = "";
0023      [Parameter] public string Value { get; set; } = "";
0024      [Parameter] public EventCallback<string> ValueChanged { get; set; }
0025      [Parameter] public string Help { get; set; } = "";
0026      [Parameter] public bool Area { get; set; }
0027      [Parameter] public bool Wide { get; set; }
0028      [Parameter] public int Rows { get; set; } = 3;
0029      [Parameter] public int MaximumLength { get; set; } = 4000;
0030      [Parameter] public string InputMode { get; set; } = "text";
0031      [Parameter] public bool TrackDirty { get; set; } = true;
0032
0033      private async Task ChangeAsync(ChangeEventArgs args)
0034      {
0035          await ValueChanged.InvokeAsync(args.Value?.ToString() ?? "");
0036          if (TrackDirty)
0037          {
0038              Workspace.MarkEditorDirty();
0039          }
0040      }
0041  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.App/Components/Shared/UploadBoundary.cs
================================================================================
0001  using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Components.Forms;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003
0004  namespace AcousticSeal.App.Components.Shared;
0005
0006  public static class UploadBoundary
0007  {
0008      public static async Task<byte[]> ReadAsync(IBrowserFile file, int maximum)
0009      {
0010          if (file.Size == 0 || file.Size > maximum)
0011          {
0012              throw new DomainValidationException("file", $"文件应为1字节至{maximum / 1024 / 1024}MiB");
0013          }
0014
0015          await using var stream = file.OpenReadStream(maxAllowedSize: maximum);
0016          using var memory = new MemoryStream();
0017          await stream.CopyToAsync(memory);
0018          if (memory.Length > maximum)
0019          {
0020              throw new DomainValidationException("file", "读取结果超过允许大小");
0021          }
0022
0023          return memory.ToArray();
0024      }
0025  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.App/Components/Shared/VmComponent.cs
================================================================================
0001  using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Components;
0002  using AcousticSeal.App.ViewModels;
0003
0004  namespace AcousticSeal.App.Components.Shared;
0005
0006  public abstract class VmComponent : ComponentBase, IDisposable
0007  {
0008      [Inject]
0009      protected WorkspaceViewModel Vm { get; set; } = null!;
0010
0011      protected override void OnInitialized()
0012      {
0013          Vm.Changed += Changed;
0014      }
0015
0016      private void Changed()
0017      {
0018          _ = InvokeAsync(StateHasChanged);
0019      }
0020
0021      public void Dispose()
0022      {
0023          Vm.Changed -= Changed;
0024          GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
0025      }
0026  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.App/DownloadVault.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Collections.Concurrent;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0003
0004  namespace AcousticSeal.App;
0005
0006  public sealed class DownloadVault
0007  {
0008      private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, DownloadEntry> entries = new();
0009
0010      public string Add(ExportDocument document)
0011      {
0012          Purge();
0013          if (document.Bytes.Length > 24 * 1024 * 1024)
0014          {
0015              throw new IOException("单个导出超过24MiB，请缩小项目范围");
0016          }
0017
0018          while (entries.Count >= 20)
0019          {
0020              var oldest = entries.OrderBy(item => item.Value.Expires).FirstOrDefault();
0021              entries.TryRemove(oldest.Key, out _);
0022          }
0023
0024          var id = Guid.NewGuid();
0025          entries[id] = new DownloadEntry(document, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddMinutes(10));
0026          return "/download/" + id.ToString("N");
0027      }
0028
0029      public ExportDocument? Find(Guid id)
0030      {
0031          Purge();
0032          return entries.TryGetValue(id, out var value) ? value.Document : null;
0033      }
0034
0035      private void Purge()
0036      {
0037          var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
0038          foreach (var (id, value) in entries)
0039          {
0040              if (value.Expires < now)
0041              {
0042                  entries.TryRemove(id, out _);
0043              }
0044          }
0045      }
0046
0047      private sealed record DownloadEntry(ExportDocument Document, DateTimeOffset Expires);
0048  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.App/Program.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Net;
0002  using AcousticSeal.App;
0003  using AcousticSeal.App.Components;
0004  using AcousticSeal.App.ViewModels;
0005  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0006  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0007
0008  var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
0009  var port = builder.Configuration.GetValue<int?>("port") ?? 8765;
0010  if (port is < 1024 or > 65535)
0011  {
0012      throw new ArgumentException("端口允许1024至65535");
0013  }
0014
0015  builder.WebHost.UseUrls("http://127.0.0.1:" + port);
0016  var dataDirectory = builder.Configuration["data-directory"] ??
0017      Path.Combine(builder.Environment.ContentRootPath, "Data");
0018  builder.Services.AddSingleton(new ProjectStore(dataDirectory));
0019  builder.Services.AddSingleton<DownloadVault>();
0020  builder.Services.AddScoped<WorkspaceViewModel>();
0021  builder.Services.AddRazorComponents().AddInteractiveServerComponents(options =>
0022  {
0023      options.DetailedErrors = builder.Environment.IsDevelopment();
0024      options.DisconnectedCircuitRetentionPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10);
0025  }).AddHubOptions(options => options.MaximumReceiveMessageSize = 128 * 1024);
0026  builder.Services.Configure<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.Features.FormOptions>(options =>
0027      options.MultipartBodyLengthLimit = Software.MaximumFileBytes);
0028
0029  var app = builder.Build();
0030  app.Use(async (context, next) =>
0031  {
0032      var host = context.Request.Host.Host;
0033      var remote = context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress;
0034      if (host is not ("127.0.0.1" or "localhost") || remote is null || !IPAddress.IsLoopback(remote))
0035      {
0036          context.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden;
0037          await context.Response.WriteAsync("仅允许本机回环访问");
0038          return;
0039      }
0040
0041      var originText = context.Request.Headers.Origin.ToString();
0042      if (originText.Length > 0 && (!Uri.TryCreate(originText, UriKind.Absolute, out var origin) ||
0043          origin.Host != host || origin.Port != port || origin.Scheme != "http"))
0044      {
0045          context.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden;
0046          await context.Response.WriteAsync("拒绝跨站访问");
0047          return;
0048      }
0049
0050      context.Response.Headers.XContentTypeOptions = "nosniff";
0051      context.Response.Headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer";
0052      context.Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";
0053      context.Response.Headers.ContentSecurityPolicy = "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self' ws://127.0.0.1:" + port + " ws://localhost:" + port + "; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'";
0054      await next();
0055  });
0056  if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
0057  {
0058      app.UseExceptionHandler("/error");
0059  }
0060
0061  app.UseAntiforgery();
0062  app.MapStaticAssets();
0063  app.MapGet("/health", () => Results.Json(new
0064  {
0065      name = Software.Name,
0066      version = Software.Version,
0067      schema = Software.SchemaVersion,
0068      status = "ready"
0069  }));
0070  app.MapGet("/download/{id:guid}", (Guid id, DownloadVault vault) =>
0071  {
0072      var document = vault.Find(id);
0073      return document is null ? Results.NotFound("下载已过期，请重新准备导出") :
0074          Results.File(document.Bytes, document.ContentType, document.Filename);
0075  });
0076  app.MapRazorComponents<App>().AddInteractiveServerRenderMode();
0077  app.Logger.LogInformation("{Name} {Version}; project directory: {Directory}", Software.Name, Software.DisplayVersion, dataDirectory);
0078  app.Run();

0061      {
0062          IsExisting = true;
0063          RequireSampleRateMatch = settings.RequireSampleRateMatch;
0064          RequireSynchronizationMatch = settings.RequireSynchronizationMatch;
0065          Inputs.AddRange(settings.Describe().Select(setting => new SettingInput
0066          {
0067              Key = setting.Key,
0068              Label = setting.Label,
0069              Unit = setting.Unit,

0073              Value = InputNumber.Format(setting.Value)
0074          }));

0076
0077      public AnalysisSettings ToModel()
0078      {
0079          var values = Inputs.ToDictionary(input => input.Key,
0080              input => InputNumber.Required(input.Value, input.Label, input.Minimum, input.Maximum));
0081          return new AnalysisSettings
0082          {
0083              SpeedTolerance = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.SpeedTolerance)],
0084              RpmTolerance = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.RpmTolerance)],
0085              TemperatureTolerance = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.TemperatureTolerance)],
0086              DurationTolerance = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.DurationTolerance)],
0087              DriftThreshold = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.DriftThreshold)],
0088              RecoveryThreshold = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.RecoveryThreshold)],
0089              RepeatabilityThreshold = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.RepeatabilityThreshold)],
0090              MinimumEffectDisplay = values[nameof(AnalysisSettings.MinimumEffectDisplay)],
0091              RequireSampleRateMatch = RequireSampleRateMatch,
0092              RequireSynchronizationMatch = RequireSynchronizationMatch
0093          };
0094      }
0095
0096      public void ResetDefaults()
0097      {
0098          foreach (var input in Inputs)
0099          {
0100              input.Value = InputNumber.Format(input.Default);
0101          }
0102

0104          RequireSynchronizationMatch = false;

0180  public sealed class AnalysisFilterViewModel : ObservableObject
0181  {
0182      public string MinimumFrequency { get; set; } = "";
0183      public string MaximumFrequency { get; set; } = "";
0184      public PairPurpose? Purpose { get; set; }
0185      public SourceKind? Source { get; set; }
0186      public ResultStatus? Status { get; set; }
0187      public Guid? Location { get; set; }
0188      public string Search { get; set; } = "";
0189      public double? AppliedMinimum { get; private set; }
0190      public double? AppliedMaximum { get; private set; }
0191      public string AppliedDescription => "频带范围：" + (AppliedMinimum?.ToString() ?? "不限") + "—" + (AppliedMaximum?.ToString() ?? "不限") + " Hz";
0192
0193      public void ApplyRange()
0194      {
0195          var low = InputNumber.Optional(MinimumFrequency, "扫描频带下限", 1, 100000);
0196          var high = InputNumber.Optional(MaximumFrequency, "扫描频带上限", 1, 100000);
0197          if (low > high)
0198          {
0199              throw new DomainValidationException("scan", "扫描下限不得大于上限");
0200          }
0201
0202          AppliedMinimum = low;
0203          AppliedMaximum = high;
0204          Notify();
0205      }
0206
0207      public void Reset()
0208      {
0209          MinimumFrequency = "";
0210          MaximumFrequency = "";
0211          AppliedMinimum = null;
0212          AppliedMaximum = null;
0213          Purpose = null;
0214          Source = null;
0215          Status = null;
0216          Location = null;
0217          Search = "";
0218          Notify();
0219      }
0220
0221      public bool IncludesFrequency(double frequency)
0222      {
0223          return (!AppliedMinimum.HasValue || frequency >= AppliedMinimum) &&
0224              (!AppliedMaximum.HasValue || frequency <= AppliedMaximum);
0225      }
0226
0227      public List<PairResult> Pairs(AnalysisReport report)
0228      {
0229          return report.Pairs.Where(pair =>
0230              (!Purpose.HasValue || pair.Purpose == Purpose) &&
0231              (!Source.HasValue || pair.Source == Source) &&
0232              (!Status.HasValue || pair.Status == Status) &&
0233              (!Location.HasValue || pair.LocationIds.Contains(Location.Value)) &&
0234              (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Search) || pair.Title.Contains(Search.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))).ToList();
0235      }
0236
0237      public List<BandDifference> Bands(PairResult pair)
0238      {
0239          return pair.Bands.Where(band => IncludesFrequency(band.Frequency)).ToList();
0240      }
0241
0242      public List<double> MatrixFrequencies(IEnumerable<PairResult> pairs)
0243      {
0244          return pairs.SelectMany(pair => pair.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency))
0245              .Distinct().Where(IncludesFrequency).Order().ToList();
0246      }
0247
0248      public PairResult ChartPair(PairResult pair)
0249      {
0250          return new PairResult
0251          {
0252              PairId = pair.PairId,
0253              BaselineName = pair.BaselineName,
0254              TargetName = pair.TargetName,
0255              Source = pair.Source,
0256              Status = pair.Status,
0257              Bands = Bands(pair)
0258          };
0259      }
0260  }

0001  using System.ComponentModel;

0006
0007  public abstract class ObservableObject : INotifyPropertyChanged
0008  {
0009      public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
0010      public event Action? Changed;
0011
0012      protected void Notify([CallerMemberName] string? property = null)
0013      {
0014          PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(property));
0015          Changed?.Invoke();
0016      }
0017  }
0018
0019  public abstract class EditorViewModel : ObservableObject
0020  {
0021      public bool IsDirty { get; private set; }
0022      public Guid Id { get; set; } = Guid.NewGuid();
0023      public bool IsExisting { get; protected set; }
0024      public string IdentityLabel => IsExisting ? Id.ToString() : "应用后建立对象";
0025
0026      public void MarkDirty()
0027      {
0028          IsDirty = true;
0029          Notify(nameof(IsDirty));
0030      }
0031
0032      public void MarkClean()
0033      {
0034          IsDirty = false;
0035          Notify(nameof(IsDirty));
0036      }
0037
0038      protected static string Text(string? value)
0039      {
0040          return value?.Trim() ?? "";
0041      }
0042  }

0176  public sealed class TrialEditor : EditorViewModel
0177  {
0178      public string Name { get; set; } = "";
0179      public TrialKind Kind { get; set; }
0180      public Guid ConditionId { get; set; }
0181      public HashSet<Guid> LocationIds { get; } = [];
0182      public string Timestamp { get; set; } = DateTimeOffset.Now.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
0183      public string RepeatGroup { get; set; } = "";
0184      public SourceKind Source { get; set; } = SourceKind.Manual;
0185      public string SourceNote { get; set; } = "";
0186      public string OriginalFile { get; set; } = "";
0187      public string Operator { get; set; } = "";
0188      public string Preparation { get; set; } = "";
0189      public string Notes { get; set; } = "";
0190      public List<BandRowEditor> Bands { get; } = [];
0191      public string PasteText { get; set; } = "";
0192
0193      public TrialEditor(AcousticTrial? item = null, Guid? defaultCondition = null)
0194      {
0195          ConditionId = defaultCondition ?? Guid.Empty;
0196          if (item is null)
0197          {
0198              AddBand(markDirty: false);
0199              return;
0200          }
0201
0202          Id = item.Id;
0203          IsExisting = true;
0204          Name = item.Name;
0205          Kind = item.Kind;
0206          ConditionId = item.ConditionId;
0207          LocationIds.UnionWith(item.LocationIds);
0208          Timestamp = item.Timestamp.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
0209          RepeatGroup = item.RepeatGroup;
0210          Source = item.Source;
0211          SourceNote = item.SourceNote;
0212          OriginalFile = item.OriginalFile;
0213          Operator = item.Operator;
0214          Preparation = item.Preparation;
0215          Notes = item.Notes;
0216          Bands.AddRange(item.Bands.OrderBy(band => band.Frequency).Select(band => new BandRowEditor
0217          {
0218              Frequency = InputNumber.Format(band.Frequency),
0219              Level = InputNumber.Format(band.Level),
0220              Note = band.Note
0221          }));
0222      }
0223
0224      public void ToggleLocation(Guid id, bool selected)
0225      {
0226          if (selected)
0227          {
0228              LocationIds.Add(id);
0229          }
0230          else
0231          {
0232              LocationIds.Remove(id);
0233          }
0234
0235          MarkDirty();
0236      }
0237
0238      public void AddBand(bool markDirty = true)
0239      {
0240          if (Bands.Count >= Software.MaximumBands)
0241          {
0242              throw new DomainValidationException("bands", "每试验最多200频带");
0243          }
0244
0245          Bands.Add(new BandRowEditor());
0246          if (markDirty)
0247          {
0248              MarkDirty();
0249          }
0250      }
0251
0252      public void RemoveBand(Guid id)
0253      {
0254          Bands.RemoveAll(row => row.RowId == id);
0255          MarkDirty();
0256      }
0257
0258      public void RemoveSelectedBands()
0259      {
0260          Bands.RemoveAll(row => row.Selected);
0261          MarkDirty();
0262      }
0263
0264      public void PasteBands()
0265      {
0266          var rows = CsvParser.Read(PasteText, ',');
0267          var parsed = new List<BandRowEditor>();
0268          foreach (var row in rows)
0269          {
0270              if (row.Cells.Count != 2)
0271              {
0272                  throw new DomainValidationException("粘贴第" + row.Line + "行", "须为Hz,声级两列，不带表头");
0273              }
0274
0275              var frequency = InputNumber.Required(row.Cells[0], "粘贴频率第" + row.Line + "行", 1, 100000);
0276              var value = row.Cells[1].Trim();
0277              var level = value is "" or "NA" or "N/A" or "null" ? null : InputNumber.Optional(value, "粘贴声级第" + row.Line + "行", -100, 200);
0278              parsed.Add(new BandRowEditor { Frequency = InputNumber.Format(frequency), Level = InputNumber.Format(level) });
0279          }
0280
0281          if (parsed.Count == 0 || parsed.Count > Software.MaximumBands ||
0282              parsed.GroupBy(row => row.Frequency).Any(group => group.Count() > 1))
0283          {
0284              throw new DomainValidationException("paste", "频带为空、重复或超过200项，原表格未改变");
0285          }
0286
0287          Bands.Clear();
0288          Bands.AddRange(parsed);
0289          MarkDirty();
0290      }
0291
0292      public AcousticTrial ToModel()
0293      {
0294          if (!Regex.IsMatch(Timestamp.Trim(), "(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant) ||
0295              !DateTimeOffset.TryParse(Timestamp, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var timestamp))
0296          {
0297              throw new DomainValidationException("timestamp", "请输入带时区ISO日期，例如2026-01-01T10:00:00+08:00");
0298          }
0299
0300          var bands = new List<BandMeasurement>();
0301          for (var index = 0; index < Bands.Count; index++)
0302          {
0303              var row = Bands[index];
0304              if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(row.Frequency) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(row.Level) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(row.Note))
0305              {
0306                  continue;
0307              }
0308
0309              bands.Add(new BandMeasurement
0310              {
0311                  Frequency = InputNumber.Required(row.Frequency, "频带第" + (index + 1) + "行频率", 1, 100000),
0312                  Level = InputNumber.Optional(row.Level, "频带第" + (index + 1) + "行声级", -100, 200),
0313                  Note = Text(row.Note)
0314              });
0315          }
0316
0317          return new AcousticTrial
0318          {
0319              Id = Id,
0320              Name = Text(Name),
0321              Kind = Kind,
0322              ConditionId = ConditionId,
0323              LocationIds = LocationIds.ToList(),
0324              Timestamp = timestamp,
0325              RepeatGroup = Text(RepeatGroup),
0326              Source = Source,
0327              SourceNote = Text(SourceNote),
0328              OriginalFile = Text(OriginalFile),
0329              Operator = Text(Operator),
0330              Preparation = Text(Preparation),
0331              Notes = Text(Notes),
0332              Bands = bands.OrderBy(band => band.Frequency).ToList()
0333          };
0334      }
0335  }
0336
0337  public static class InputNumber
0338  {
0339      public static double? Optional(string value, string path, double minimum, double maximum)
0340      {
0341          if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
0342          {
0343              return null;
0344          }
0345
0346          return Required(value, path, minimum, maximum);
0347      }
0348
0349      public static double Required(string value, string path, double minimum, double maximum)
0350      {
0351          if (!double.TryParse(value.Trim(), NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var number) ||
0352              !double.IsFinite(number) || number < minimum || number > maximum)
0353          {
0354              throw new DomainValidationException(path, $"请输入{minimum}至{maximum}的有限数值，使用句点小数");
0355          }
0356
0357          return number;
0358      }
0359
0360      public static string Format(double? value)
0361      {
0362          return value?.ToString("G15", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "";
0363      }
0364  }

src/AcousticSeal.App/ViewModels/RevisionViewModel.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Text.Json;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0005
0006  namespace AcousticSeal.App.ViewModels;
0007
0008  public sealed class RevisionViewModel : ObservableObject
0009  {
0010      private AcousticProject? left;
0011      private AcousticProject? right;
0012      public string LeftFilename { get; private set; } = "";
0013      public string RightFilename { get; private set; } = "";
0014      public string LeftIdentity => Describe(left);
0015      public string RightIdentity => Describe(right);
0016      public List<string> MigrationNotes { get; private set; } = [];
0017      public RevisionComparisonResult? Result { get; private set; }
0018      public string Search { get; set; } = "";
0019      public ChangeKind? Kind { get; set; }
0020      public int Page { get; private set; }
0021      public string DownloadUrl { get; private set; } = "";
0022      public string DownloadFilename { get; private set; } = "";
0023      public bool CanCompare => left is not null && right is not null;
0024      public int PageSize => 50;
0025
0026      public void Load(bool isLeft, byte[] bytes, string filename)
0027      {
0028          var decoded = new ProjectCodec().Decode(bytes);
0029          if (isLeft)
0030          {
0031              left = decoded.Project;
0032              LeftFilename = filename;
0033          }
0034          else
0035          {
0036              right = decoded.Project;
0037              RightFilename = filename;
0038          }
0039
0040          MigrationNotes = decoded.Notes;
0041          Result = null;
0042          DownloadUrl = "";
0043          Notify();
0044      }
0045
0046      public void UseCurrent(bool isLeft, AcousticProject project)
0047      {
0048          if (isLeft)
0049          {
0050              left = ProjectCodec.Clone(project);
0051              LeftFilename = "当前已应用工作副本（快照）";
0052          }
0053          else
0054          {
0055              right = ProjectCodec.Clone(project);
0056              RightFilename = "当前已应用工作副本（快照）";
0057          }
0058
0059          Result = null;
0060          DownloadUrl = "";
0061          Notify();
0062      }
0063
0064      public void Compare()
0065      {
0066          if (left is null || right is null)
0067          {
0068              throw new DomainValidationException("compare", "先载入左右两份项目或使用当前快照");
0069          }
0070
0071          Result = new RevisionComparison().Compare(left, right);
0072          Page = 0;
0073          Notify();
0074      }
0075
0076      public List<RevisionChange> Filtered()
0077      {
0078          return Result?.Changes.Where(change => (!Kind.HasValue || change.Kind == Kind) &&
0079              (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Search) || (change.Path + change.Before + change.After)
0080                  .Contains(Search.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))).ToList() ?? [];
0081      }
0082
0083      public List<RevisionChange> Visible()
0084      {
0085          return Filtered().Skip(Page * PageSize).Take(PageSize).ToList();
0086      }
0087
0088      public void Filter()
0089      {
0090          Page = 0;
0091          Notify();
0092      }
0093
0094      public void MovePage(int change)
0095      {
0096          var maximum = Math.Max(0, (Filtered().Count - 1) / PageSize);
0097          Page = Math.Clamp(Page + change, 0, maximum);
0098          Notify();
0099      }
0100
0101      public void Export(DownloadVault vault)
0102      {
0103          if (Result is null)
0104          {
0105              throw new DomainValidationException("compare", "先执行内容核对");
0106          }
0107
0108          DownloadFilename = "项目版本核对.analysis.json";
0109          DownloadUrl = vault.Add(new ExportDocument(DownloadFilename, "application/json; charset=utf-8",
0110              JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(Result, ProjectCodec.Options)));
0111          Notify();
0112      }
0113
0114      public void Clear()
0115      {
0116          left = null;
0117          right = null;
0118          LeftFilename = "";
0119          RightFilename = "";
0120          Result = null;
0121          MigrationNotes = [];
0122          DownloadUrl = "";
0123          Search = "";
0124          Kind = null;
0125          Page = 0;
0126          Notify();
0127      }
0128
0129      public static string KindText(ChangeKind kind)
0130      {
0131          return kind switch
0132          {
0133              ChangeKind.Added => "新增",
0134              ChangeKind.Removed => "移除",
0135              _ => "修改"
0136          };
0137      }
0138
0139      private static string Describe(AcousticProject? project)
0140      {
0141          return project is null ? "尚未载入" : project.Name + " · 修订" + project.Revision + " · " + project.Id;
0142      }
0143  }

src/AcousticSeal.App/ViewModels/ScenarioViewModel.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.App.ViewModels;
0006
0007  public sealed class ScenarioViewModel : ObservableObject
0008  {
0009      public string Parameter { get; set; } = nameof(AnalysisSettings.DriftThreshold);
0010      public string Values { get; set; } = "0.2,0.5,1";
0011      public ScenarioComparison? Result { get; private set; }
0012      public string DownloadUrl { get; private set; } = "";
0013      public string AppliedRequest { get; private set; } = "";
0014      public bool DraftChanged => AppliedRequest != Parameter + ":" + Values;
0015
0016      public void Calculate(AcousticProject project)
0017      {
0018          var setting = project.Settings.Describe().First(item => item.Key == Parameter);
0019          var parts = Values.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
0020          var values = parts.Select((text, index) => InputNumber.Required(text,
0021              "情景参数第" + (index + 1) + "项", setting.Minimum, setting.Maximum)).ToList();
0022          Result = new SettingsScenarios().Compare(project, Parameter, values);
0023          AppliedRequest = Parameter + ":" + Values;
0024          DownloadUrl = "";
0025          Notify();
0026      }
0027
0028      public bool IsStale(AcousticProject project)
0029      {
0030          return Result is not null && Result.InputHash != ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project);
0031      }
0032
0033      public void Export(AcousticProject project, DownloadVault vault)
0034      {
0035          if (Result is null || IsStale(project) || DraftChanged)
0036          {
0037              throw new DomainValidationException("scenario", "情景结果为空或输入已改变，请重新计算后下载");
0038          }
0039
0040          DownloadUrl = vault.Add(new SettingsScenarios().Export(Result));
0041          Notify();
0042      }
0043
0044      public void Clear()
0045      {
0046          Result = null;
0047          DownloadUrl = "";
0048          AppliedRequest = "";
0049          Notify();
0050      }
0051  }

0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.App.ViewModels;
0006
0007  public sealed class WorkbookViewModel : ObservableObject
0008  {
0009      public HashSet<Guid> Selected { get; } = [];
0010      public string Minimum { get; set; } = "";
0011      public string Maximum { get; set; } = "";
0012      public string Search { get; set; } = "";
0013      public InputWorkbookResult? Result { get; private set; }
0014      public string DownloadUrl { get; private set; } = "";
0015      public string InputHash { get; private set; } = "";
0016      public long Revision { get; private set; }
0017
0018      public void Toggle(Guid id, bool selected)
0019      {
0020          if (selected)
0021          {
0022              Selected.Add(id);
0023          }
0024          else
0025          {
0026              Selected.Remove(id);
0027          }
0028
0029          Result = null;
0030          DownloadUrl = "";
0031          Notify();
0032      }
0033
0034      public void Build(AcousticProject project)
0035      {
0036          var minimum = InputNumber.Optional(Minimum, "核对频带下限", 1, 100000);
0037          var maximum = InputNumber.Optional(Maximum, "核对频带上限", 1, 100000);
0038          Result = new InputWorkbook().Build(project, Selected.ToList(), minimum, maximum);
0039          InputHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project);
0040          Revision = project.Revision;
0041          DownloadUrl = "";
0042          Notify();
0043      }
0044
0045      public List<AcousticTrial> Candidates(AcousticProject project)
0046      {
0047          return project.Trials.Where(trial => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Search) ||
0048              (trial.Name + trial.RepeatGroup).Contains(Search.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).Take(100).ToList();
0049      }
0050
0051      public bool IsStale(AcousticProject project)
0052      {
0053          return Result is not null && InputHash != ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project);
0054      }
0055
0056      public void Export(AcousticProject project, DownloadVault vault)
0057      {
0058          if (Result is null || IsStale(project))
0059          {
0060              throw new DomainValidationException("workbook", "工作表为空或项目已改变，请重新生成核对表");
0061          }
0062
0063          DownloadUrl = vault.Add(new ExportDocument("原始输入核对表.csv", "text/csv; charset=utf-8", new InputWorkbook().Csv(Result)));
0064          Notify();
0065      }
0066
0067      public void Clear()
0068      {
0069          Selected.Clear();
0070          Minimum = "";
0071          Maximum = "";
0072          Search = "";
0073          Result = null;
0074          InputHash = "";
0075          DownloadUrl = "";
0076          Notify();
0077      }
0078  }

0266      public void DeleteSelected(string kind, Guid id)

0276                  case "condition":

0279                      break;
0280                  case "trial":
0281                      ProjectCommands.DeleteTrial(Session, id);
0282                      selectedTrial = null;
0283                      break;
0284                  case "pair":
0285                      ProjectCommands.DeletePair(Session, id);
0286                      selectedPair = null;
0287                      break;
0288                  default:
0289                      throw new DomainValidationException("delete", "未知对象类型");
0290              }

0293          }));

0337
0338      public async Task SaveAsync()
0339      {
0340          if (!EnsureApplied())
0341          {
0342              return;
0343          }
0344
0345          await RunAsync(async () =>
0346          {
0347              var receipt = await store.SaveAsync(Project, Session.SavedETag);
0348              Session.MarkSaved(receipt);
0349              LastSavedPath = receipt.Path;
0350              LastBackupPath = receipt.BackupPath;
0351              Library = await store.ListAsync();
0352              Message("项目实际保存成功：" + receipt.Path);
0353          });
0354      }
0355
0356      public void OpenStored(Guid id, bool backup = false)
0357      {
0358          AskAsync(backup ? "从备份打开" : "打开本机项目", "将替换当前工作副本。" +
0359              (HasUnsaved ? "有未保存内容，请先取消并保存。" : "确认继续？"), async () =>
0360          {
0361              await RunAsync(async () =>
0362              {
0363                  var stored = backup ? await store.LoadBackupAsync(id) : await store.LoadAsync(id);
0364                  Session.Open(stored.Project, stored.ETag, stored.Path, changed: backup);
0365                  ResetWorkspace();
0366                  Message(backup ? "备份已打开为未保存副本，确认内容后显式保存。" : "项目已从磁盘打开：" + stored.Path);
0367              });
0368          });
0369      }

0371      public void ImportProject(byte[] bytes, string filename)
0372      {
0373          Run(() =>
0374          {
0375              var decoded = new ProjectCodec().Decode(bytes);
0376              Ask("导入项目文件", "将以“" + decoded.Project.Name + "”替换工作副本。原文件不会被修改；保存前可复制为新项目标识。" +
0377                  (HasUnsaved ? "当前有未保存内容，确认前请取消并先保存。" : ""), () =>
0378              {
0379                  Session.Open(decoded.Project, path: filename, changed: true);
0380                  ResetWorkspace();
0381                  MigrationNotes = decoded.Notes;
0382                  Message("已导入" + filename + (decoded.Migrated ? "并完成内存迁移，请另存新格式。" : "；本机已有同标识时须先打开磁盘版本或复制项目再保存。"));
0383              });
0384          });
0385      }

0399              Message("已读取CSV到预检区，尚未修改测量。");
0400          });

0408              Import.SetPreview(preview);
0409              Message(preview.CanApply ? "CSV预检通过，请核对覆盖范围再确认导入。" : "CSV预检发现错误，整批不可应用。", !preview.CanApply);
0410          });

0413      public void ApplyCsv()
0414      {
0415          if (Import.Preview is not { } preview)
0416          {
0417              Message("请先预检CSV。", true);
0418              return;
0419          }
0420
0421          Ask("确认CSV整体导入", $"{preview.Rows}行、{preview.Changes.Count}个试验、覆盖{preview.Replaced}个同频带、缺失{preview.Missing}个。模式：{preview.Mode}。来源沿用试验档案。", () => Run(() =>
0422          {
0423              csv.Apply(Session, preview);
0424              AfterChange("CSV已整体应用，错误或过期预检不会改动数据。");
0425          }));

0433              Message("批量配对预检完成，请逐行核对。共享基线不作为独立配对估计区间。");
0434          });

0437      public void ApplyBatch()
0438      {
0439          if (Batch.Preview is not { } preview)
0440          {
0441              Message("先选择基线和目标并预检。", true);
0442              return;
0443          }
0444
0445          Ask("确认显式批量配对", "将建立" + preview.Rows.Count + "条明确对应关系；条件不符的配对保留阻断原因。", () => Run(() =>
0446          {
0447              pairing.Apply(Session, preview);
0448              Batch = Track(new BatchPairEditor());
0449              AfterChange("批量配对已建立，未按名称或时间自动推断。");
0450          }));
0451      }

0455          ReviewDraftDirty = true;
0456          Notify();
0457      }

0459      public void RecordReview()
0460      {
0461          if (!EnsureApplied(allowReview: true))
0462          {
0463              return;
0464          }
0465
0466          Run(() =>
0467          {
0468              Session.RecordReview(Reviewer, ReviewComment);
0469              ReviewDraftDirty = false;
0470              AfterChange("已记录输入修订、参数、哈希和阻断原因；这是用户复核记录，不是认证证书。");
0471          });
0472      }
0473
0474      public void PrepareExport(string kind)
0475      {
0476          if (!EnsureApplied())
0477          {
0478              return;
0479          }
0480
0481          Run(() =>
0482          {
0483              var document = kind switch
0484              {
0485                  "project" => export.Project(Project),
0486                  "measurements" => export.Measurements(Project),
0487                  "results" => export.Results(Project, Analysis),
0488                  "analysis" => export.AnalysisJson(Project, Analysis),
0489                  "report" => export.Report(Project, Analysis),
0490                  _ => throw new DomainValidationException("export", "导出类型无效")
0491              };
0492              DownloadUrl = downloads.Add(document);
0493              DownloadFilename = document.Filename;
0494              Message("文件已准备，点击下载链接。浏览器实际下载位置由其设置决定；准备导出不会替代本机保存。");
0495          });
0496      }
0497
0498      public void NavigateIssue(QualityIssue issue)
0499      {
0500          AskDraft("定位问题前将放弃未应用表单。", () =>
0501          {
0502              ReloadEditors();
0503              switch (issue.EntityType)
0504              {
0505                  case "condition":
0506                      Page = "conditions";
0507                      selectedCondition = issue.EntityId;
0508                      Condition = Track(new ConditionEditor(Project.Conditions.Single(item => item.Id == issue.EntityId)));
0509                      break;
0510                  case "trial":
0511                      Page = "trials";
0512                      selectedTrial = issue.EntityId;
0513                      Trial = Track(new TrialEditor(Project.Trials.Single(item => item.Id == issue.EntityId)));
0514                      break;
0515                  case "pair":
0516                      Page = "pairs";
0517                      selectedPair = issue.EntityId;
0518                      Pair = Track(new PairEditor(Project.Pairs.Single(item => item.Id == issue.EntityId)));
0519                      break;
0520                  default:
0521                      Page = "project";
0522                      break;
0523              }
0524
0525              Message(issue.Repair);
0526          });
0527      }
0528
0529      public List<AcousticTrial> FilteredTrials()
0530      {
0531          return Project.Trials.Where(trial => (!TrialFilter.HasValue || trial.Kind == TrialFilter) &&
0532              (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(TrialSearch) || (trial.Name + trial.Id + trial.RepeatGroup)
0533                  .Contains(TrialSearch.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))).ToList();
0534      }
0535
0536      public List<AcousticTrial> VisibleTrials()
0537      {
0538          return FilteredTrials().Skip(TrialPage * PageSize).Take(PageSize).ToList();
0539      }
0540
0541      public void ApplyTrialFilter()
0542      {
0543          TrialPage = 0;
0544          Notify();
0545      }
0546
0547      public void NextTrials(int change)
0548      {
0549          var pages = Math.Max(1, (FilteredTrials().Count + PageSize - 1) / PageSize);
0550          TrialPage = Math.Clamp(TrialPage + change, 0, pages - 1);
0551          Notify();
0552      }
0553
0554      public List<QualityIssue> VisibleIssues()
0555      {
0556          return Analysis.Issues.Where(issue => QualitySeverity == "all" || issue.Severity.ToString() == QualitySeverity).ToList();
0557      }
0558
0559      public void ApplyScan()
0560      {
0561          Run(Filter.ApplyRange);
0562      }
0563
0564      public void Edit(Action action)
0565      {
0566          Run(action);
0567      }
0568
0569      public void ClearNotice()
0570      {
0571          Notice = null;
0572          Problems = [];
0573          Notify();
0574      }
0575
0576      public void CancelConfirmation()
0577      {
0578          confirmedAction = null;
0579          Confirmation = "";
0580          Notify();
0581      }
0582
0583      public async Task ConfirmAsync()
0584      {
0585          var action = confirmedAction;
0586          confirmedAction = null;
0587          Confirmation = "";
0588          if (action is not null)
0589          {
0590              await action();
0591          }
0592
0593          Notify();
0594      }
0595
0596      private void AfterChange(string message, bool reload = true)
0597      {
0598          if (reload)
0599          {
0600              ReloadEditors();
0601          }
0602
0603          Import.ClearPreview();
0604          Message(message + " 当前工作副本尚未保存。");
0605      }
0606
0607      private void ResetWorkspace()
0608      {
0609          selectedLocation = null;
0610          selectedCondition = null;
0611          selectedTrial = null;
0612          selectedPair = null;
0613          Page = "project";
0614          TrialPage = 0;
0615          TrialSearch = "";
0616          TrialFilter = null;
0617          MigrationNotes = [];
0618          ReviewDraftDirty = false;
0619          Reviewer = "";
0620          ReviewComment = "";
0621          DownloadUrl = "";
0622          DownloadFilename = "";
0623          Import = Track(new ImportEditor());
0624          Batch = Track(new BatchPairEditor());
0625          Filter.Reset();
0626          Workbook.Clear();
0627          Scenario.Clear();
0628          ReloadEditors();
0629          Notify();
0630      }
0631
0632      private void ReloadEditors()
0633      {
0634          Metadata = Track(new MetadataEditor(Project));
0635          Location = Track(new LocationEditor(Project.Locations.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Id == selectedLocation)));
0636          Condition = Track(new ConditionEditor(Project.Conditions.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Id == selectedCondition)));
0637          Trial = Track(new TrialEditor(Project.Trials.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Id == selectedTrial), Project.Conditions.FirstOrDefault()?.Id));
0638          Pair = Track(new PairEditor(Project.Pairs.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Id == selectedPair)));
0639          Settings = Track(new SettingsEditor(Project.Settings));
0640      }
0641
0642      private EditorViewModel? ActiveEditor()
0643      {
0644          return Page switch
0645          {
0646              "project" => Metadata,
0647              "locations" => Location,
0648              "conditions" => Condition,
0649              "trials" => Trial,
0650              "pairs" => Pair,
0651              "quality" => Settings,
0652              "import" => Import,
0653              _ => null
0654          };
0655      }
0656
0657      private T Track<T>(T editor) where T : ObservableObject
0658      {
0659          editor.Changed += () => Notify();
0660          return editor;
0661      }
0662
0663      private bool EnsureApplied(bool allowReview = false)
0664      {
0665          if (ActiveEditor()?.IsDirty == true || (Page == "pairs" && Batch.IsDirty) || (ReviewDraftDirty && !allowReview))
0666          {
0667              Message("表单尚未应用。先应用修改或取消草稿，再保存、复核或导出。", true);
0668              return false;
0669          }
0670
0671          return true;
0672      }
0673
0674      private void AskDraft(string text, Action action)
0675      {
0676          if (HasDraft)
0677          {
0678              Ask("未应用表单", text, action);
0679          }
0680          else
0681          {
0682              action();
0683          }
0684      }
0685
0686      private void AskReplace(string title, Action action)
0687      {
0688          if (HasUnsaved)
0689          {
0690              Ask(title, "当前工作副本有未保存内容。请先取消并保存；确认后将替换工作副本。", action);
0691          }
0692          else
0693          {
0694              action();
0695          }
0696      }
0697
0698      private void Ask(string title, string message, Action action)
0699      {
0700          AskAsync(title, message, () =>
0701          {
0702              action();
0703              return Task.CompletedTask;
0704          });
0705      }
0706
0707      private void AskAsync(string title, string message, Func<Task> action)
0708      {
0709          ConfirmationTitle = title;
0710          Confirmation = message;
0711          confirmedAction = action;
0712          Notify();
0713      }
0714
0715      private void Message(string text, bool error = false)
0716      {
0717          Notice = new UserNotice(text, error);
0718          Notify();
0719      }
0720
0721      private void Run(Action task)
0722      {
0723          if (Busy)
0724          {
0725              return;
0726          }
0727
0728          Problems = [];
0729          try
0730          {
0731              task();
0732          }
0733          catch (DomainValidationException error)
0734          {
0735              Problems = error.Problems.ToList();
0736              Message("操作未应用。请按字段提示修正，原项目数据保留。", true);
0737          }
0738          catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ProjectConflictException)
0739          {
0740              Message(error.Message, true);
0741          }
0742
0743          Notify();
0744      }
0745
0746      private async Task RunAsync(Func<Task> task)
0747      {
0748          if (Busy)
0749          {
0750              return;
0751          }
0752
0753          Busy = true;
0754          Problems = [];
0755          Notify();
0756          try
0757          {
0758              await task();
0759          }
0760          catch (DomainValidationException error)
0761          {
0762              Problems = error.Problems.ToList();
0763              Message("操作未完成，输入未覆盖。", true);
0764          }
0765          catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ProjectConflictException)
0766          {
0767              Message(error.Message, true);
0768          }
0769          finally
0770          {
0771              Busy = false;
0772              Notify();
0773          }
0774      }
0775  }
