================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/AcousticMath.cs
================================================================================
0001  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0002
0003  public static class AcousticMath
0004  {
0005      private static readonly double[] Critical95 =
0006      [
0007          12.706204736, 4.302652730, 3.182446305, 2.776445105, 2.570581836,
0008          2.446911851, 2.364624252, 2.306004135, 2.262157163, 2.228138852,
0009          2.200985160, 2.178812830, 2.160368656, 2.144786688, 2.131449546,
0010          2.119905299, 2.109815578, 2.100922040, 2.093024054, 2.085963447,
0011          2.079613845, 2.073873068, 2.068657610, 2.063898562, 2.059538553,
0012          2.055529439, 2.051830516, 2.048407142, 2.045229642, 2.042272456
0013      ];
0014
0015      public static double? EnergyMean(IEnumerable<double> input)
0016      {
0017          var values = Checked(input);
0018          if (values.Count == 0)
0019          {
0020              return null;
0021          }
0022
0023          var maximum = values.Max();
0024          var sum = CompensatedSum(values.Select(value => Math.Pow(10, (value - maximum) / 10)));
0025          return maximum + 10 * Math.Log10(sum / values.Count);
0026      }
0027
0028      public static double? EnergySum(IEnumerable<double> input)
0029      {
0030          var values = Checked(input);
0031          if (values.Count == 0)
0032          {
0033              return null;
0034          }
0035
0036          var maximum = values.Max();
0037          var sum = CompensatedSum(values.Select(value => Math.Pow(10, (value - maximum) / 10)));
0038          return maximum + 10 * Math.Log10(sum);
0039      }
0040
0041      public static double? Mean(IEnumerable<double> input)
0042      {
0043          var values = Checked(input);
0044          return values.Count == 0 ? null : CompensatedSum(values) / values.Count;
0045      }
0046
0047      public static double? SampleStandardDeviation(IEnumerable<double> input)
0048      {
0049          var values = Checked(input);
0050          if (values.Count < 2)
0051          {
0052              return null;
0053          }
0054
0055          var mean = Mean(values)!.Value;
0056          var sum = CompensatedSum(values.Select(value => (value - mean) * (value - mean)));
0057          return Math.Sqrt(sum / (values.Count - 1));
0058      }
0059
0060      public static DescriptiveStatistics Describe(IEnumerable<double> input)
0061      {
0062          var values = Checked(input);
0063          if (values.Count == 0)
0064          {
0065              return new DescriptiveStatistics();
0066          }
0067
0068          return new DescriptiveStatistics
0069          {
0070              Count = values.Count,
0071              ArithmeticMean = Mean(values),
0072              EnergyMean = EnergyMean(values),
0073              StandardDeviation = SampleStandardDeviation(values),
0074              Minimum = values.Min(),
0075              Maximum = values.Max(),
0076              Range = values.Max() - values.Min()
0077          };
0078      }
0079
0080      public static PairedStatistics Paired(
0081          IReadOnlyList<double> baseline,
0082          IReadOnlyList<double> target,
0083          bool independent)
0084      {
0085          if (baseline.Count != target.Count)
0086          {
0087              throw new ArgumentException("配对基线与目标数量须相同");
0088          }
0089
0090          var differences = baseline.Zip(target, (before, after) => before - after).ToList();
0091          var mean = Mean(differences);
0092          var deviation = SampleStandardDeviation(differences);
0093          double? lower = null;
0094          double? upper = null;
0095          var note = "n<2，不生成置信区间";
0096          if (differences.Count > 1 && !independent)
0097          {
0098              note = "同组配对复用试验，独立性不满足，不生成置信区间";
0099          }
0100          else if (differences.Count > 1)
0101          {
0102              var freedom = differences.Count - 1;
0103              var critical = Critical95[Math.Min(freedom, 30) - 1];
0104              var half = critical * deviation!.Value / Math.Sqrt(differences.Count);
0105              lower = mean - half;
0106              upper = mean + half;
0107              note = "独立配对差近似正态的双侧95% Student t均值区间；" +
0108                  (freedom > 30 ? "df>30保守使用df=30" : "df=" + freedom) +
0109                  "；不含仪器系统不确定性";
0110          }
0111
0112          return new PairedStatistics
0113          {
0114              Count = differences.Count,
0115              MeanDifference = mean,
0116              StandardDeviation = deviation,
0117              GroupEnergyMeanDifference = baseline.Count == 0 ? null : EnergyMean(baseline) - EnergyMean(target),
0118              Lower95 = lower,
0119              Upper95 = upper,
0120              IntervalNote = note
0121          };
0122      }
0123
0124      private static List<double> Checked(IEnumerable<double> values)
0125      {
0126          var list = values.ToList();
0127          if (list.Any(value => !double.IsFinite(value)))
0128          {
0129              throw new ArgumentException("计算输入须为有限数值；缺失值应在明确口径后筛选");
0130          }
0131
0132          return list;
0133      }
0134
0135      private static double CompensatedSum(IEnumerable<double> values)
0136      {
0137          double total = 0;
0138          double correction = 0;
0139          foreach (var value in values)
0140          {
0141              var adjusted = value - correction;
0142              var next = total + adjusted;
0143              correction = (next - total) - adjusted;
0144              total = next;
0145          }
0146
0147          return total;
0148      }
0149  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/AnalysisEngine.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0002
0003  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0004
0005  public sealed class AnalysisEngine
0006  {
0007      private readonly ProjectValidator validator = new();
0008      private readonly Comparability comparability = new();
0009
0010      public AnalysisReport Calculate(AcousticProject project)
0011      {
0012          validator.Ensure(project);
0013          var conditions = project.Conditions.ToDictionary(condition => condition.Id);
0014          var trials = project.Trials.ToDictionary(trial => trial.Id);
0015          var pairs = project.Pairs.Select(pair => Compare(pair, trials, conditions, project.Settings)).ToList();
0016          return new AnalysisReport
0017          {
0018              ProjectId = project.Id,
0019              Revision = project.Revision,
0020              Pairs = pairs,
0021              Effects = Summarize(pairs, trials, conditions, project.Settings),
0022              Repeatability = Repeatability(project, conditions),
0023              Coverage = BuildCoverage(project, pairs),
0024              Issues = new QualityInspector().Inspect(project, pairs)
0025          };
0026      }
0027
0028      private PairResult Compare(
0029          TrialPair pair,
0030          Dictionary<Guid, AcousticTrial> trials,
0031          Dictionary<Guid, MeasurementCondition> conditions,
0032          AnalysisSettings settings)
0033      {
0034          var baseline = trials[pair.BaselineId];
0035          var target = trials[pair.TargetId];
0036          var left = conditions[baseline.ConditionId];
0037          var right = conditions[target.ConditionId];
0038          var reasons = comparability.Inspect(left, right, settings);
0039          var warnings = new List<string>();
0040          if (baseline.Source != target.Source)
0041          {
0042              reasons.Add("来源类别不同，禁止混合模拟、实测、人工或估算作为同一配对");
0043          }
0044
0045          if (baseline.Source != SourceKind.Measured)
0046          {
0047              warnings.Add("包含" + baseline.Source.Text() + "数据，不能证明真实应用效果");
0048          }
0049
0050          var elapsed = (target.Timestamp - baseline.Timestamp).TotalSeconds;
0051          if (elapsed < 0)
0052          {
0053              warnings.Add("目标时间早于基线，核对时间与配对用途");
0054          }
0055
0056          var frequencies = Frequencies([baseline, target]);
0057          var bands = new List<BandDifference>();
0058          foreach (var frequency in frequencies)
0059          {
0060              var before = baseline.LevelAt(frequency);
0061              var after = target.LevelAt(frequency);
0062              var status = reasons.Count > 0 ? ResultStatus.Blocked :
0063                  !before.HasValue || !after.HasValue ? ResultStatus.Missing : ResultStatus.Valid;
0064              var difference = status == ResultStatus.Valid ? before - after : null;
0065              var interpretation = difference switch
0066              {
0067                  null => status.Text(),
0068                  > 0 => "目标声级低于基线",
0069                  < 0 => "目标声级高于基线",
0070                  _ => "两者声级相同"
0071              };
0072              if (difference.HasValue && Math.Abs(difference.Value) < settings.MinimumEffectDisplay)
0073              {
0074                  interpretation += "；低于用户变化标记阈值";
0075              }
0076
0077              bands.Add(new BandDifference
0078              {
0079                  Frequency = frequency,
0080                  Baseline = before,
0081                  Target = after,
0082                  Difference = difference,
0083                  Status = status,
0084                  Interpretation = interpretation
0085              });
0086          }
0087
0088          if (bands.Any(band => band.Status == ResultStatus.Missing))
0089          {
0090              warnings.Add("部分频带缺失，仅计算共同有效频带，不补零");
0091          }
0092
0093          var validCount = bands.Count(band => band.Difference.HasValue);
0094          var baseTotal = Total(baseline, left);
0095          var targetTotal = Total(target, right);
0096          var sameFrequencies = baseline.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency).Order().SequenceEqual(
0097              target.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency).Order());
0098          var totalNote = "只表示已录入频带范围的能量和，不是全频总声级";
0099          double? totalDifference = null;
0100          if (reasons.Count > 0)
0101          {
0102              totalNote = "条件或来源不可比，不比较合成声级";
0103          }
0104          else if (!sameFrequencies)
0105          {
0106              totalNote = "频带集合不同，不比较合成声级";
0107          }
0108          else if (!left.NonOverlappingBands || !right.NonOverlappingBands)
0109          {
0110              totalNote = "未声明频带互不重叠，不计算合成声级";
0111          }
0112          else if (!baseTotal.HasValue || !targetTotal.HasValue)
0113          {
0114              totalNote = "频带缺失或为空，不计算合成声级";
0115          }
0116          else
0117          {
0118              totalDifference = baseTotal - targetTotal;
0119          }
0120
0121          ScreeningResult? screening = null;
0122          if (pair.Purpose != PairPurpose.Effect)
0123          {
0124              var threshold = pair.Purpose == PairPurpose.Drift ? settings.DriftThreshold : settings.RecoveryThreshold;
0125              var worst = bands.Where(band => band.Difference.HasValue)
0126                  .OrderByDescending(band => Math.Abs(band.Difference!.Value)).FirstOrDefault();
0127              var maximum = worst is null ? (double?)null : Math.Abs(worst.Difference!.Value);
0128              screening = new ScreeningResult(
0129                  threshold,
0130                  maximum,
0131                  worst?.Frequency,
0132                  maximum is null ? ResultStatus.Empty : maximum > threshold ? ResultStatus.Alert : ResultStatus.Within);
0133          }
0134
0135          return new PairResult
0136          {
0137              PairId = pair.Id,
0138              BaselineId = baseline.Id,
0139              TargetId = target.Id,
0140              BaselineName = baseline.Name,
0141              TargetName = target.Name,
0142              Purpose = pair.Purpose,
0143              TargetKind = target.Kind,
0144              Source = baseline.Source,
0145              LocationIds = [.. target.LocationIds],
0146              Status = reasons.Count > 0 ? ResultStatus.Blocked : validCount > 0 ? ResultStatus.Valid : ResultStatus.Empty,
0147              BlockingReasons = reasons,
0148              Warnings = warnings,
0149              Bands = bands,
0150              BaselineTotal = baseTotal,
0151              TargetTotal = targetTotal,
0152              TotalDifference = totalDifference,
0153              TotalNote = totalNote,
0154              ElapsedSeconds = elapsed,
0155              Screening = screening,
0156              BaselineSource = baseline.SourceNote,
0157              TargetSource = target.SourceNote
0158          };
0159      }
0160
0161      private List<EffectSummary> Summarize(
0162          List<PairResult> pairs,
0163          Dictionary<Guid, AcousticTrial> trials,
0164          Dictionary<Guid, MeasurementCondition> conditions,
0165          AnalysisSettings settings)
0166      {
0167          var groups = pairs.Where(pair => pair.Purpose == PairPurpose.Effect && pair.Status == ResultStatus.Valid)
0168              .GroupBy(pair => Key(pair.LocationIds) + "|" + pair.Source);
0169          var summaries = new List<EffectSummary>();
0170          foreach (var group in groups)
0171          {
0172              var entries = group.ToList();
0173              var reasons = new List<string>();
0174              // Check all pairs, rather than only one anchor, so tolerances do not
0175              // silently accumulate through a nontransitive matching chain.
0176              for (var index = 0; index < entries.Count; index++)
0177              {
0178                  for (var other = index + 1; other < entries.Count; other++)
0179                  {
0180                      reasons.AddRange(comparability.Inspect(
0181                          conditions[trials[entries[index].BaselineId].ConditionId],
0182                          conditions[trials[entries[other].BaselineId].ConditionId], settings));
0183                  }
0184              }
0185
0186              var ids = entries.SelectMany(pair => new[] { pair.BaselineId, pair.TargetId }).ToList();
0187              var independent = ids.Distinct().Count() == ids.Count;
0188              var bands = new List<EffectBandSummary>();
0189              foreach (var frequency in entries.SelectMany(pair => pair.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency)).Distinct().Order())
0190              {
0191                  var valid = entries.SelectMany(pair => pair.Bands)
0192                      .Where(band => band.Frequency == frequency && band.Difference.HasValue).ToList();
0193                  var statistics = reasons.Count > 0
0194                      ? new PairedStatistics { Count = valid.Count, IntervalNote = "跨配对工况不同，禁止汇总" }
0195                      : AcousticMath.Paired(
0196                          valid.Select(band => band.Baseline!.Value).ToList(),
0197                          valid.Select(band => band.Target!.Value).ToList(), independent);
0198                  bands.Add(new EffectBandSummary { Frequency = frequency, Statistics = statistics });
0199              }
0200
0201              summaries.Add(new EffectSummary
0202              {
0203                  Key = group.Key,
0204                  LocationIds = [.. entries[0].LocationIds],
0205                  Source = entries[0].Source,
0206                  IndependentPairs = independent,
0207                  PairIds = entries.Select(pair => pair.PairId).ToList(),
0208                  BlockingReasons = reasons.Distinct().ToList(),
0209                  Bands = bands
0210              });
0211          }
0212
0213          return summaries;
0214      }
0215
0216      private List<RepeatabilityGroup> Repeatability(AcousticProject project, Dictionary<Guid, MeasurementCondition> conditions)
0217      {
0218          var groups = project.Trials.Where(trial => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(trial.RepeatGroup))
0219              .GroupBy(trial => (trial.RepeatGroup, trial.Kind, Locations: Key(trial.LocationIds), trial.Source));
0220          var output = new List<RepeatabilityGroup>();
0221          foreach (var group in groups)
0222          {
0223              var entries = group.ToList();
0224              var reasons = comparability.MissingFields(conditions[entries[0].ConditionId], project.Settings);
0225              for (var index = 0; index < entries.Count; index++)
0226              {
0227                  for (var other = index + 1; other < entries.Count; other++)
0228                  {
0229                      reasons.AddRange(comparability.Inspect(conditions[entries[index].ConditionId],
0230                          conditions[entries[other].ConditionId], project.Settings));
0231                  }
0232              }
0233
0234              var bands = new List<RepeatabilityBand>();
0235              foreach (var frequency in Frequencies(entries))
0236              {
0237                  var levels = entries.Select(trial => trial.LevelAt(frequency)).Where(value => value.HasValue)
0238                      .Select(value => value!.Value).ToList();
0239                  var statistics = reasons.Count > 0 ? new DescriptiveStatistics { Count = levels.Count } : AcousticMath.Describe(levels);
0240                  bands.Add(new RepeatabilityBand
0241                  {
0242                      Frequency = frequency,
0243                      MissingCount = entries.Count - levels.Count,
0244                      Statistics = statistics,
0245                      Status = reasons.Count > 0 ? ResultStatus.Blocked : levels.Count < 2 ? ResultStatus.Insufficient :
0246                          statistics.StandardDeviation > project.Settings.RepeatabilityThreshold ? ResultStatus.Alert : ResultStatus.Within
0247                  });
0248              }
0249
0250              output.Add(new RepeatabilityGroup
0251              {
0252                  Name = group.Key.RepeatGroup,
0253                  Kind = group.Key.Kind,
0254                  Source = group.Key.Source,
0255                  LocationIds = [.. entries[0].LocationIds],
0256                  TrialIds = entries.Select(trial => trial.Id).ToList(),
0257                  BlockingReasons = reasons.Distinct().ToList(),
0258                  Threshold = project.Settings.RepeatabilityThreshold,
0259                  Bands = bands
0260              });
0261          }
0262
0263          return output;
0264      }
0265
0266      private CoverageReport BuildCoverage(AcousticProject project, List<PairResult> pairs)
0267      {
0268          return new CoverageReport
0269          {
0270              TrialTypes = Enum.GetValues<TrialKind>().ToDictionary(kind => kind, kind => project.Trials.Count(trial => trial.Kind == kind)),
0271              Conditions = project.Conditions.Select(condition => new ConditionCoverage(condition.Id, condition.Name,
0272                  project.Trials.Count(trial => trial.ConditionId == condition.Id), comparability.MissingFields(condition, project.Settings))).ToList(),
0273              Locations = project.Locations.Select(location => new LocationCoverage(
0274                  location.Id, location.Name,
0275                  project.Trials.Count(trial => trial.Kind == TrialKind.Single && trial.LocationIds.Contains(location.Id)),
0276                  project.Trials.Count(trial => trial.Kind == TrialKind.Combination && trial.LocationIds.Contains(location.Id)),
0277                  pairs.Count(pair => pair.Purpose == PairPurpose.Effect && pair.Status == ResultStatus.Valid && pair.LocationIds.Contains(location.Id)))).ToList(),
0278              Frequencies = Frequencies(project.Trials).Select(frequency =>
0279              {
0280                  var declared = project.Trials.Where(trial => trial.Bands.Any(band => band.Frequency == frequency)).ToList();
0281                  var valid = declared.Count(trial => trial.LevelAt(frequency).HasValue);
0282                  return new FrequencyCoverage(frequency, valid, declared.Count - valid, project.Trials.Count - declared.Count);
0283              }).ToList(),
0284              Timeline = project.Trials.OrderBy(trial => trial.Timestamp).Select(trial => new TimelineEntry(
0285                  trial.Id, trial.Name, trial.Kind, trial.Source, trial.Timestamp, trial.ValidBandCount,
0286                  pairs.Count(pair => pair.TargetId == trial.Id))).ToList()
0287          };
0288      }
0289
0290      public static List<double> Frequencies(IEnumerable<AcousticTrial> trials)
0291      {
0292          return trials.SelectMany(trial => trial.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency)).Distinct().Order().ToList();
0293      }
0294
0295      public static string Key(IEnumerable<Guid> ids)
0296      {
0297          return string.Join("+", ids.Order());
0298      }
0299
0300      public static double? Total(AcousticTrial trial, MeasurementCondition condition)
0301      {
0302          if (!condition.NonOverlappingBands || trial.Bands.Count == 0 || trial.Bands.Any(band => !band.Level.HasValue))
0303          {
0304              return null;
0305          }
0306
0307          return AcousticMath.EnergySum(trial.Bands.Select(band => band.Level!.Value));
0308      }
0309  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/Comparability.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0002
0003  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0004
0005  public sealed class Comparability
0006  {
0007      public List<string> Inspect(MeasurementCondition left, MeasurementCondition right, AnalysisSettings settings)
0008      {
0009          var reasons = new List<string>();
0010          var rightFields = right.TextFields().ToDictionary(field => field.Key);
0011          foreach (var field in left.TextFields())
0012          {
0013              var other = rightFields[field.Key];
0014              CompareText(field.Label, field.Value, other.Value, reasons);
0015          }
0016
0017          CompareNumber("车速", "km/h", left.Speed, right.Speed, settings.SpeedTolerance, reasons);
0018          CompareNumber("转速", "r/min", left.Rpm, right.Rpm, settings.RpmTolerance, reasons);
0019          CompareNumber("温度", "°C", left.Temperature, right.Temperature, settings.TemperatureTolerance, reasons);
0020          CompareNumber("积分时长", "s", left.Duration, right.Duration, settings.DurationTolerance, reasons);
0021          if (left.NonOverlappingBands != right.NonOverlappingBands)
0022          {
0023              reasons.Add("互不重叠频带声明不同");
0024          }
0025
0026          if (settings.RequireSampleRateMatch)
0027          {
0028              CompareNumber("采样率", "Hz", left.SampleRate, right.SampleRate, 0, reasons);
0029          }
0030
0031          if (settings.RequireSynchronizationMatch)
0032          {
0033              CompareText("时钟与同步定义", left.Synchronization, right.Synchronization, reasons);
0034          }
0035
0036          return reasons;
0037      }
0038
0039      public List<string> MissingFields(MeasurementCondition condition, AnalysisSettings settings)
0040      {
0041          return Inspect(condition, condition, settings);
0042      }
0043
0044      private static void CompareText(string label, string? left, string? right, List<string> reasons)
0045      {
0046          if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(left) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(right))
0047          {
0048              reasons.Add(label + "缺失，无法确认可比性");
0049          }
0050          else if (!string.Equals(left.Trim(), right.Trim(), StringComparison.Ordinal))
0051          {
0052              reasons.Add(label + "不同：" + left + " / " + right);
0053          }
0054      }
0055
0056      private static void CompareNumber(string label, string unit, double? left, double? right, double tolerance, List<string> reasons)
0057      {
0058          if (!left.HasValue || !right.HasValue)
0059          {
0060              reasons.Add(label + "缺失，无法确认可比性");
0061              return;
0062          }
0063
0064          var difference = Math.Abs(left.Value - right.Value);
0065          if (difference > tolerance + 1e-10)
0066          {
0067              reasons.Add($"{label}绝对差{difference:G6} {unit}超过容差{tolerance:G6}");
0068          }
0069      }
0070  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/InputWorkbook.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Text;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0006
0007  public sealed record WorkbookColumn(Guid TrialId, string Name, string Condition, SourceKind Source, string SourceNote);
0008  public sealed record WorkbookCell(double? Level, bool Declared, string Note)
0009  {
0010      public string Status => !Declared ? "未声明" : Level.HasValue ? "有效" : "明确缺失";
0011  }
0012  public sealed record WorkbookRow(double Frequency, List<WorkbookCell> Cells);
0013
0014  public sealed class InputWorkbookResult
0015  {
0016      public List<WorkbookColumn> Columns { get; init; } = [];
0017      public List<WorkbookRow> Rows { get; init; } = [];
0018      public List<string> Warnings { get; init; } = [];
0019      public int CompleteRows => Rows.Count(row => row.Cells.All(cell => cell.Level.HasValue));
0020      public string Purpose { get; init; } = "原始频带人工核对，不自动配对、不计算封堵差、不证明跨列可比。";
0021  }
0022
0023  public sealed class InputWorkbook
0024  {
0025      public InputWorkbookResult Build(AcousticProject project, IReadOnlyList<Guid> ids, double? minimum, double? maximum)
0026      {
0027          if (ids.Count == 0 || ids.Count > 20 || ids.Distinct().Count() != ids.Count)
0028          {
0029              throw new DomainValidationException("workbook", "请显式选择1至20条不重复试验");
0030          }
0031
0032          if (minimum > maximum || minimum is < 1 or > 100000 || maximum is < 1 or > 100000)
0033          {
0034              throw new DomainValidationException("workbook.frequency", "频带范围无效");
0035          }
0036
0037          var trials = new List<AcousticTrial>();
0038          foreach (var id in ids)
0039          {
0040              trials.Add(project.Trials.FirstOrDefault(trial => trial.Id == id)
0041                  ?? throw new DomainValidationException("workbook.trial", "选中的试验已不存在，请重新选择"));
0042          }
0043
0044          var rows = new List<WorkbookRow>();
0045          foreach (var frequency in AnalysisEngine.Frequencies(trials).Where(frequency =>
0046              (!minimum.HasValue || frequency >= minimum) && (!maximum.HasValue || frequency <= maximum)))
0047          {
0048              var cells = new List<WorkbookCell>();
0049              foreach (var trial in trials)
0050              {
0051                  var band = trial.Bands.FirstOrDefault(band => band.Frequency == frequency);
0052                  cells.Add(new WorkbookCell(band?.Level, band is not null, band?.Note ?? ""));
0053              }
0054
0055              rows.Add(new WorkbookRow(frequency, cells));
0056          }
0057
0058          var warnings = new List<string>();
0059          if (trials.Select(trial => trial.Source).Distinct().Count() > 1)
0060          {
0061              warnings.Add("所选试验包含不同来源类别，只供原始输入核对，不能混合统计。");
0062          }
0063
0064          var conditions = project.Conditions.ToDictionary(condition => condition.Id);
0065          var comparator = new Comparability();
0066          for (var index = 0; index < trials.Count; index++)
0067          {
0068              for (var other = index + 1; other < trials.Count; other++)
0069              {
0070                  var reasons = comparator.Inspect(conditions[trials[index].ConditionId], conditions[trials[other].ConditionId], project.Settings);
0071                  if (reasons.Count > 0)
0072                  {
0073                      warnings.Add(trials[index].Name + " / " + trials[other].Name + "：" + string.Join("；", reasons));
0074                  }
0075              }
0076          }
0077
0078          return new InputWorkbookResult
0079          {
0080              Columns = trials.Select(trial => new WorkbookColumn(trial.Id, trial.Name,
0081                  conditions[trial.ConditionId].Name, trial.Source, trial.SourceNote)).ToList(),
0082              Rows = rows,
0083              Warnings = warnings.Distinct().ToList()
0084          };
0085      }
0086
0087      public byte[] Csv(InputWorkbookResult workbook)
0088      {
0089          var output = new StringBuilder("\uFEFF");
0090          var header = new List<string> { "frequency_hz" };
0091          foreach (var column in workbook.Columns)
0092          {
0093              header.Add(column.TrialId + "_level_db");
0094              header.Add(column.TrialId + "_state");
0095          }
0096
0097          output.AppendLine(string.Join(",", header));
0098          foreach (var row in workbook.Rows)
0099          {
0100              var cells = new List<string> { row.Frequency.ToString("G17", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) };
0101              foreach (var cell in row.Cells)
0102              {
0103                  cells.Add(cell.Level?.ToString("G17", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "");
0104                  cells.Add(cell.Status);
0105              }
0106
0107              output.AppendLine(string.Join(",", cells));
0108          }
0109
0110          output.AppendLine();
0111          output.AppendLine("trial_id,name,condition,source_kind,source_note");
0112          foreach (var column in workbook.Columns)
0113          {
0114              output.AppendLine(string.Join(",", new[] { column.TrialId.ToString(), column.Name, column.Condition, column.Source.Text(), column.SourceNote }
0115                  .Select(CsvCell)));
0116          }
0117
0118          return Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(output.ToString());
0119      }
0120
0121      private static string CsvCell(string text)
0122      {
0123          if (text.Length > 0 && "=+-@\t\r".Contains(text[0]))
0124          {
0125              text = "'" + text;
0126          }
0127
0128          return "\"" + text.Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
0129      }
0130  }

src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/QualityInspector.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0002
0003  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0004
0005  public sealed class QualityInspector
0006  {
0007      public List<QualityIssue> Inspect(AcousticProject project, List<PairResult> results)
0008      {
0009          var issues = new List<QualityIssue>();
0010          var pairedIds = project.Pairs.SelectMany(pair => new[] { pair.BaselineId, pair.TargetId }).ToHashSet();
0011          var comparability = new Comparability();
0012          foreach (var condition in project.Conditions)
0013          {
0014              foreach (var missing in comparability.MissingFields(condition, project.Settings))
0015              {
0016                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "condition.incomplete", "condition", condition.Id,
0017                      condition.Name, missing, "打开工况，按原始试验记录补充该字段后应用；不要用零代替未知"));
0018              }
0019          }
0020
0021          foreach (var trial in project.Trials)
0022          {
0023              if (trial.ValidBandCount == 0)
0024              {
0025                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "trial.empty", "trial", trial.Id,
0026                      trial.Name, "没有有效频带", "打开试验频带表，录入声级或通过CSV预检导入"));
0027              }
0028
0029              if (trial.MissingBandCount > 0)
0030              {
0031                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "trial.missing", "trial", trial.Id,
0032                      trial.Name, $"存在{trial.MissingBandCount}个明确缺失频带", "核对仪器导出记录；确实缺失时保留空值，不补零"));
0033              }
0034
0035              if (!pairedIds.Contains(trial.Id))
0036              {
0037                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Information, "trial.unpaired", "trial", trial.Id,
0038                      trial.Name, "尚未被显式配对引用", "按试验计划建立基线与目标对应关系，或说明保留为参考数据"));
0039              }
0040
0041              if (trial.Source != SourceKind.Measured)
0042              {
0043                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Information, "trial.source", "trial", trial.Id,
0044                      trial.Name, "数据来源为" + trial.Source.Text(), "输出时继续保留来源标识；本记录不能证明真实车辆应用效果"));
0045              }
0046          }
0047
0048          foreach (var result in results)
0049          {
0050              foreach (var reason in result.BlockingReasons)
0051              {
0052                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Error, "pair.blocked", "pair", result.PairId,
0053                      result.Title, reason, "打开配对核对基线与目标，再进入对应工况或试验按真实记录修正"));
0054              }
0055
0056              foreach (var warning in result.Warnings)
0057              {
0058                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "pair.warning", "pair", result.PairId,
0059                      result.Title, warning, "展开配对来源和时间详情，确认输入或保留说明"));
0060              }
0061
0062              if (result.Screening?.Status == ResultStatus.Alert)
0063              {
0064                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "screening.alert", "pair", result.PairId,
0065                      result.Title, "最大频带绝对差超过用户" + result.Purpose.Text() + "阈值",
0066                      "检查封堵恢复状态、仪器标定、声源稳定性和环境条件，再决定是否补测；不要仅改阈值消除警示"));
0067              }
0068          }
0069
0070          foreach (var purpose in new[] { PairPurpose.Drift, PairPurpose.Recovery })
0071          {
0072              if (project.Pairs.All(pair => pair.Purpose != purpose))
0073              {
0074                  issues.Add(new QualityIssue(IssueSeverity.Warning, "coverage." + purpose, "project", project.Id,
0075                      project.Name, "尚无" + purpose.Text() + "配对", "先采集对应试验，再明确指定基线和目标；不能推断稳定或已恢复"));
0076              }
0077          }
0078
0079          return issues.OrderByDescending(issue => issue.Severity).ThenBy(issue => issue.Title, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();
0080      }
0081  }

src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/Results.cs

0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0002
0003  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0004
0005  public sealed class AnalysisReport
0006  {
0007      public Guid ProjectId { get; init; }
0008      public long Revision { get; init; }
0009      public string SoftwareVersion { get; init; } = Software.Version;
0010      public DateTimeOffset CalculatedAt { get; init; } = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
0011      public string Limitation { get; init; } = Software.Limitation;
0012      public List<PairResult> Pairs { get; init; } = [];
0013      public List<EffectSummary> Effects { get; init; } = [];
0014      public List<RepeatabilityGroup> Repeatability { get; init; } = [];
0015      public List<QualityIssue> Issues { get; init; } = [];
0016      public CoverageReport Coverage { get; init; } = new();
0017      public int ValidPairCount => Pairs.Count(pair => pair.Status == ResultStatus.Valid);
0018      public int BlockedPairCount => Pairs.Count(pair => pair.Status == ResultStatus.Blocked);
0019      public int WarningCount => Issues.Count(issue => issue.Severity == IssueSeverity.Warning);
0020  }
0021
0022  public sealed class PairResult
0023  {
0024      public Guid PairId { get; init; }
0025      public Guid BaselineId { get; init; }
0026      public Guid TargetId { get; init; }
0027      public string BaselineName { get; init; } = "";
0028      public string TargetName { get; init; } = "";
0029      public PairPurpose Purpose { get; init; }
0030      public TrialKind TargetKind { get; init; }
0031      public SourceKind Source { get; init; }
0032      public List<Guid> LocationIds { get; init; } = [];
0033      public ResultStatus Status { get; init; }
0034      public List<string> BlockingReasons { get; init; } = [];
0035      public List<string> Warnings { get; init; } = [];
0036      public List<BandDifference> Bands { get; init; } = [];
0037      public double? BaselineTotal { get; init; }
0038      public double? TargetTotal { get; init; }
0039      public double? TotalDifference { get; init; }
0040      public string TotalNote { get; init; } = "";
0041      public double ElapsedSeconds { get; init; }
0042      public ScreeningResult? Screening { get; init; }
0043      public string BaselineSource { get; init; } = "";
0044      public string TargetSource { get; init; } = "";
0045      public string Title => BaselineName + " → " + TargetName;
0046  }
0047
0048  public sealed class BandDifference
0049  {
0050      public double Frequency { get; init; }
0051      public double? Baseline { get; init; }
0052      public double? Target { get; init; }
0053      public double? Difference { get; init; }
0054      public ResultStatus Status { get; init; }
0055      public string Interpretation { get; init; } = "";
0056  }
0057
0058  public sealed record ScreeningResult(
0059      double Threshold,
0060      double? MaximumAbsoluteDifference,
0061      double? WorstFrequency,

src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/RevisionComparison.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Text.Json;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0005
0006  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0007
0008  public enum ChangeKind
0009  {
0010      Added,
0011      Removed,
0012      Modified
0013  }
0014
0015  public sealed record RevisionChange(
0016      ChangeKind Kind,
0017      string Collection,
0018      string EntityId,
0019      string Path,
0020      string Before,
0021      string After);
0022
0023  public sealed class RevisionComparisonResult
0024  {
0025      public string SoftwareVersion { get; init; } = Software.Version;
0026      public Guid LeftProjectId { get; init; }
0027      public Guid RightProjectId { get; init; }
0028      public string LeftName { get; init; } = "";
0029      public string RightName { get; init; } = "";
0030      public long LeftRevision { get; init; }
0031      public long RightRevision { get; init; }
0032      public string LeftHash { get; init; } = "";
0033      public string RightHash { get; init; } = "";
0034      public List<string> Notes { get; init; } = [];
0035      public List<RevisionChange> Changes { get; init; } = [];
0036      public int AddedCount => Changes.Count(change => change.Kind == ChangeKind.Added);
0037      public int RemovedCount => Changes.Count(change => change.Kind == ChangeKind.Removed);
0038      public int ModifiedCount => Changes.Count(change => change.Kind == ChangeKind.Modified);
0039  }
0040
0041  public sealed class RevisionComparison
0042  {
0043      public RevisionComparisonResult Compare(AcousticProject left, AcousticProject right)
0044      {
0045          var validator = new ProjectValidator();
0046          validator.Ensure(left);
0047          validator.Ensure(right);
0048          using var before = JsonDocument.Parse(JsonSerializer.Serialize(left, ProjectCodec.Options));
0049          using var after = JsonDocument.Parse(JsonSerializer.Serialize(right, ProjectCodec.Options));
0050          var changes = new List<RevisionChange>();
0051          var ignored = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
0052          {
0053              "audit", "reviews", "createdAt", "updatedAt", "softwareVersion", "revision", "id", "schemaVersion"
0054          };
0055          foreach (var property in before.RootElement.EnumerateObject())
0056          {
0057              if (!ignored.Contains(property.Name))
0058              {
0059                  Walk(property.Value, after.RootElement.GetProperty(property.Name), property.Name, "project", "", changes);
0060              }
0061          }
0062
0063          var notes = new List<string>
0064          {
0065              "只按对象GUID和频率键核对，不按名称猜测对象对应关系。",
0066              "这里是两份已录入项目内容的变化，不是封堵效果或物理路径贡献。",
0067              "审计与复核记录只比较数量，详细历史在各项目文件内查看；时间、修订和软件版本列为身份信息。"
0068          };
0069          if (left.Id != right.Id)
0070          {
0071              notes.Add("两侧项目标识不同：只核对仍具有相同GUID的对象，不能断言属于同一次试验版本。");
0072          }
0073
0074          if (left.Audit.Count != right.Audit.Count || left.Reviews.Count != right.Reviews.Count)
0075          {
0076              notes.Add($"审计数量{left.Audit.Count}→{right.Audit.Count}；复核数量{left.Reviews.Count}→{right.Reviews.Count}。");
0077          }
0078
0079          return new RevisionComparisonResult
0080          {
0081              LeftProjectId = left.Id,
0082              RightProjectId = right.Id,
0083              LeftName = left.Name,
0084              RightName = right.Name,
0085              LeftRevision = left.Revision,
0086              RightRevision = right.Revision,
0087              LeftHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(left),
0088              RightHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(right),
0089              Notes = notes,
0090              Changes = changes
0091          };
0092      }
0093
0094      private static void Walk(JsonElement left, JsonElement right, string path, string collection, string entityId, List<RevisionChange> changes)
0095      {
0096          if (left.ValueKind != right.ValueKind)
0097          {
0098              Add(ChangeKind.Modified, left, right, path, collection, entityId, changes);
0099              return;
0100          }
0101
0102          if (left.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object)
0103          {
0104              foreach (var property in left.EnumerateObject())
0105              {
0106                  if (right.TryGetProperty(property.Name, out var other))
0107                  {
0108                      Walk(property.Value, other, path + "." + property.Name, collection, entityId, changes);
0109                  }
0110                  else
0111                  {
0112                      changes.Add(new RevisionChange(ChangeKind.Removed, collection, entityId,
0113                          path + "." + property.Name, Show(property.Value), "未提供"));
0114                  }
0115              }
0116
0117              foreach (var property in right.EnumerateObject())
0118              {
0119                  if (!left.TryGetProperty(property.Name, out _))
0120                  {
0121                      changes.Add(new RevisionChange(ChangeKind.Added, collection, entityId,
0122                          path + "." + property.Name, "未提供", Show(property.Value)));
0123                  }
0124              }
0125
0126              return;
0127          }
0128
0129          if (left.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
0130          {
0131              var a = left.EnumerateArray().ToList();
0132              var b = right.EnumerateArray().ToList();
0133              var keyed = a.Concat(b).All(item => item.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && Key(item) is not null);
0134              if (keyed)
0135              {
0136                  var leftMap = a.ToDictionary(item => Key(item)!);
0137                  var rightMap = b.ToDictionary(item => Key(item)!);
0138                  foreach (var key in leftMap.Keys.Union(rightMap.Keys).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal))
0139                  {
0140                      var foundLeft = leftMap.TryGetValue(key, out var first);
0141                      var foundRight = rightMap.TryGetValue(key, out var second);
0142                      var location = path + "[" + key + "]";
0143                      if (!foundLeft)
0144                      {
0145                          changes.Add(new RevisionChange(ChangeKind.Added, path, key, location, "未提供", Show(second)));
0146                      }
0147                      else if (!foundRight)
0148                      {
0149                          changes.Add(new RevisionChange(ChangeKind.Removed, path, key, location, Show(first), "未提供"));
0150                      }
0151                      else
0152                      {
0153                          Walk(first, second, location, path, key, changes);
0154                      }
0155                  }
0156              }
0157              else
0158              {
0159                  var leftValues = a.Select(item => item.GetRawText()).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal);
0160                  var rightValues = b.Select(item => item.GetRawText()).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal);
0161                  if (!leftValues.SequenceEqual(rightValues))
0162                  {
0163                      Add(ChangeKind.Modified, left, right, path, collection, entityId, changes);
0164                  }
0165              }
0166
0167              return;
0168          }
0169
0170          if (left.GetRawText() != right.GetRawText())
0171          {
0172              Add(ChangeKind.Modified, left, right, path, collection, entityId, changes);
0173          }
0174      }
0175
0176      private static string? Key(JsonElement value)
0177      {
0178          if (value.TryGetProperty("id", out var id))
0179          {
0180              return id.GetString();
0181          }
0182
0183          if (value.TryGetProperty("frequency", out var frequency))
0184          {
0185              return frequency.GetDouble().ToString("G17", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
0186          }
0187
0188          return null;
0189      }
0190
0191      private static void Add(ChangeKind kind, JsonElement before, JsonElement after, string path,
0192          string collection, string entityId, List<RevisionChange> changes)
0193      {
0194          changes.Add(new RevisionChange(kind, collection, entityId, path, Show(before), Show(after)));
0195      }
0196
0197      private static string Show(JsonElement value)
0198      {
0199          return value.ValueKind switch
0200          {
0201              JsonValueKind.Null => "缺失(null)",
0202              JsonValueKind.String => value.GetString() ?? "",
0203              _ => value.GetRawText()
0204          };
0205      }
0206  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/SettingsScenarios.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Text.Json;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0006
0007  public sealed record ScenarioPairState(Guid PairId, string Title, ResultStatus Status, ResultStatus? Screening, List<string> Reasons);
0008  public sealed record SettingsScenario(double Value, int ValidPairs, int BlockedPairs, int ScreeningAlerts, int RepeatabilityAlerts, List<ScenarioPairState> Pairs);
0009  public sealed record ScenarioComparison(string InputHash, string Parameter, string Label, string Unit, double Original, List<SettingsScenario> Scenarios, string Note);
0010
0011  public sealed class SettingsScenarios
0012  {
0013      public ScenarioComparison Compare(AcousticProject project, string key, IReadOnlyList<double> values)
0014      {
0015          new ProjectValidator().Ensure(project);
0016          var setting = project.Settings.Describe().FirstOrDefault(setting => setting.Key == key)
0017              ?? throw new DomainValidationException("scenario.parameter", "未知可扫描参数");
0018          if (values.Count == 0 || values.Count > 20 || values.Distinct().Count() != values.Count)
0019          {
0020              throw new DomainValidationException("scenario.values", "请提供1至20个互不重复的参数值");
0021          }
0022
0023          foreach (var value in values)
0024          {
0025              if (!double.IsFinite(value) || value < setting.Minimum || value > setting.Maximum)
0026              {
0027                  throw new DomainValidationException("scenario.values", $"{setting.Label}允许{setting.Minimum}至{setting.Maximum}{setting.Unit}");
0028              }
0029          }
0030
0031          var scenarios = new List<SettingsScenario>();
0032          foreach (var value in values)
0033          {
0034              var copy = ProjectCodec.Clone(project);
0035              Set(copy.Settings, key, value);
0036              var report = new AnalysisEngine().Calculate(copy);
0037              scenarios.Add(new SettingsScenario(
0038                  value,
0039                  report.ValidPairCount,
0040                  report.BlockedPairCount,
0041                  report.Pairs.Count(pair => pair.Screening?.Status == ResultStatus.Alert),
0042                  report.Repeatability.Sum(group => group.Bands.Count(band => band.Status == ResultStatus.Alert)),
0043                  report.Pairs.Select(pair => new ScenarioPairState(pair.PairId, pair.Title, pair.Status,
0044                      pair.Screening?.Status, pair.BlockingReasons)).ToList()));
0045          }
0046
0047          return new ScenarioComparison(ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project), key, setting.Label, setting.Unit, setting.Value, scenarios,
0048              "仅对相同输入按假设工程参数重新判读，不改测量、不推荐放宽规则、不代表标准认证或组合预测。当前项目参数保持不变。");
0049      }
0050
0051      public static void Set(AnalysisSettings settings, string key, double value)
0052      {
0053          switch (key)
0054          {
0055              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.SpeedTolerance):
0056                  settings.SpeedTolerance = value;
0057                  break;
0058              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.RpmTolerance):
0059                  settings.RpmTolerance = value;
0060                  break;
0061              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.TemperatureTolerance):
0062                  settings.TemperatureTolerance = value;
0063                  break;
0064              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.DurationTolerance):
0065                  settings.DurationTolerance = value;
0066                  break;
0067              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.DriftThreshold):
0068                  settings.DriftThreshold = value;
0069                  break;
0070              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.RecoveryThreshold):
0071                  settings.RecoveryThreshold = value;
0072                  break;
0073              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.RepeatabilityThreshold):
0074                  settings.RepeatabilityThreshold = value;
0075                  break;
0076              case nameof(AnalysisSettings.MinimumEffectDisplay):
0077                  settings.MinimumEffectDisplay = value;
0078                  break;
0079              default:
0080                  throw new DomainValidationException("scenario.parameter", "未知参数");
0081          }
0082      }
0083
0084      public ExportDocument Export(ScenarioComparison comparison)
0085      {
0086          return new ExportDocument("工程规则情景.analysis.json", "application/json; charset=utf-8",
0087              JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(comparison, ProjectCodec.Options));
0088      }
0089  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Analysis/SpectrumChart.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Text;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0006
0007  public static class SpectrumChart
0008  {
0009      public static string Build(PairResult pair)
0010      {
0011          if (pair.Status == ResultStatus.Blocked)
0012          {
0013              return "<p>配对条件已阻断，不绘制可比频谱图。原始声级仍在数值表中保留。</p>";
0014          }
0015
0016          var bands = pair.Bands.Where(band => band.Baseline.HasValue || band.Target.HasValue).ToList();
0017          if (bands.Count == 0)
0018          {
0019              return "<p>没有有效声级，无法绘制频谱。</p>";
0020          }
0021
0022          if (bands.Count > 30)
0023          {
0024              return "<p>超过30个频带，请先缩窄界面频带扫描范围，或使用完整数值表。</p>";
0025          }
0026
0027          var levels = bands.SelectMany(band => new[] { band.Baseline, band.Target }).Where(value => value.HasValue)
0028              .Select(value => value!.Value).ToList();
0029          var low = Math.Floor(levels.Min() / 5) * 5 - 5;
0030          var high = Math.Ceiling(levels.Max() / 5) * 5 + 5;
0031          var minFrequency = bands.Min(band => band.Frequency);
0032          var maxFrequency = bands.Max(band => band.Frequency);
0033          var minimumLog = Math.Log10(minFrequency);
0034          var logSpan = Math.Log10(maxFrequency) - minimumLog;
0035          double X(double frequency)
0036          {
0037              return logSpan == 0 ? 430 : 65 + (Math.Log10(frequency) - minimumLog) / logSpan * 710;
0038          }
0039
0040          double Y(double level)
0041          {
0042              return 225 - (level - low) / (high - low) * 175;
0043          }
0044
0045          var builder = new StringBuilder("<svg viewBox=\"0 0 820 300\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"基线与目标原始频带声级图，横轴对数频率Hz，纵轴声级dB\">");
0046          builder.Append("<text x=\"8\" y=\"18\" font-size=\"11\">声级 dB re 20 µPa · 截断纵轴</text>");
0047          for (var index = 0; index < 5; index++)
0048          {
0049              var value = low + index * (high - low) / 4;
0050              var y = Y(value);
0051              builder.Append("<line x1=\"60\" x2=\"790\" y1=\"").Append(C(y)).Append("\" y2=\"").Append(C(y)).Append("\" stroke=\"#dce6e8\"/>");
0052              builder.Append("<text x=\"8\" y=\"").Append(C(y + 4)).Append("\" font-size=\"10\">").Append(C(value)).Append("</text>");
0053          }
0054
0055          foreach (var band in bands)
0056          {
0057              builder.Append("<text x=\"").Append(C(X(band.Frequency))).Append("\" y=\"248\" text-anchor=\"middle\" font-size=\"10\">").Append(C(band.Frequency)).Append("</text>");
0058          }
0059
0060          DrawSeries(builder, bands, band => band.Baseline, "#617b8a", "基线", X, Y);
0061          DrawSeries(builder, bands, band => band.Target, "#138d82", "目标", X, Y);
0062          builder.Append("<text x=\"140\" y=\"278\" fill=\"#617b8a\" font-size=\"12\">● 基线</text>");
0063          builder.Append("<text x=\"240\" y=\"278\" fill=\"#138d82\" font-size=\"12\">● 目标</text>");
0064          builder.Append("<text x=\"440\" y=\"278\" font-size=\"11\">缺失处断线；连线仅引导阅读，不插值补数</text>");
0065          builder.Append("<text x=\"755\" y=\"295\" font-size=\"11\">Hz（对数）</text></svg>");
0066          return builder.ToString();
0067      }
0068
0069      private static void DrawSeries(StringBuilder output, List<BandDifference> bands, Func<BandDifference, double?> value,
0070          string color, string label, Func<double, double> x, Func<double, double> y)
0071      {
0072          var segment = new List<string>();
0073          void Flush()
0074          {
0075              if (segment.Count > 1)
0076              {
0077                  output.Append("<polyline fill=\"none\" stroke=\"").Append(color).Append("\" stroke-width=\"2\" points=\"")
0078                      .Append(string.Join(" ", segment)).Append("\"/>");
0079              }
0080
0081              segment.Clear();
0082          }
0083
0084          foreach (var band in bands)
0085          {
0086              var level = value(band);
0087              if (!level.HasValue)
0088              {
0089                  Flush();
0090                  continue;
0091              }
0092
0093              var point = C(x(band.Frequency)) + "," + C(y(level.Value));
0094              segment.Add(point);
0095              output.Append("<circle cx=\"").Append(C(x(band.Frequency))).Append("\" cy=\"").Append(C(y(level.Value)))
0096                  .Append("\" r=\"4\" fill=\"").Append(color).Append("\"><title>").Append(label).Append(" ")
0097                  .Append(C(band.Frequency)).Append("Hz ").Append(C(level.Value)).Append("dB</title></circle>");
0098          }
0099
0100          Flush();
0101      }
0102
0103      private static string C(double value)
0104      {
0105          return value.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
0106      }
0107  }

0071              Notes = "所有条件值为模拟，不表示仪器已真实标定。"
0072          });
0073          AddTrial(project, 30, "基线1", TrialKind.Baseline, [], "baseline", [70, 68, 66], 0);
0074          AddTrial(project, 31, "基线2", TrialKind.Baseline, [], "baseline", [70.2, 68.1, 66.2], 1);
0075          AddTrial(project, 32, "门密封单项1", TrialKind.Single, [StableId(10)], "door", [67, 66, 65], 2);
0076          AddTrial(project, 33, "门密封单项2", TrialKind.Single, [StableId(10)], "door", [67.1, 66.2, 65.1], 3);
0077          AddTrial(project, 34, "线束孔单项", TrialKind.Single, [StableId(11)], "", [68, 66.5, 65.5], 4);
0078          AddTrial(project, 35, "两位置组合测量（模拟）", TrialKind.Combination, [StableId(10), StableId(11)], "", [65.5, 64.5, 64], 5);
0079          AddTrial(project, 36, "结束基线", TrialKind.Baseline, [], "baseline", [70.4, 68.2, 66.3], 6);
0080          AddTrial(project, 37, "解除封堵恢复", TrialKind.Recovery, [], "", [70.1, 68.2, 66.1], 7);
0081          AddPair(project, 40, 30, 32, PairPurpose.Effect);
0082          AddPair(project, 41, 31, 33, PairPurpose.Effect);
0083          AddPair(project, 42, 30, 34, PairPurpose.Effect);
0084          AddPair(project, 43, 30, 35, PairPurpose.Effect);
0085          AddPair(project, 44, 30, 36, PairPurpose.Drift);
0086          AddPair(project, 45, 30, 37, PairPurpose.Recovery);
0087          new ProjectValidator().Ensure(project);
0088          return project;
0089      }
0090
0091      private static void AddTrial(AcousticProject project, int id, string name, TrialKind kind,
0092          List<Guid> locations, string repeat, double[] levels, int minute)
0093      {
0094          project.Trials.Add(new AcousticTrial
0095          {
0096              Id = StableId(id),
0097              Name = name,
0098              Kind = kind,
0099              ConditionId = StableId(20),
0100              LocationIds = locations,
0101              Timestamp = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 1, 1, 10, minute, 0, TimeSpan.Zero),
0102              RepeatGroup = repeat,
0103              Source = SourceKind.Simulation,
0104              SourceNote = "固定模拟数列；AcousticSeal.Core/Domain/Demonstration.cs；非实测。",
0105              OriginalFile = "SIM-NO-REAL-FILE",
0106              Operator = "模拟操作员",
0107              Preparation = "模拟准备记录，不表示真实试验已执行。",
0108              Notes = "仅用于软件流程和数值核验。",
0109              Bands = new[] { 125.0, 250.0, 500.0 }.Zip(levels, (frequency, level) =>
0110                  new BandMeasurement { Frequency = frequency, Level = level }).ToList()
0111          });
0112      }
0113
0114      private static void AddPair(AcousticProject project, int id, int baseline, int target, PairPurpose purpose)
0115      {
0116          project.Pairs.Add(new TrialPair
0117          {
0118              Id = StableId(id),
0119              BaselineId = StableId(baseline),
0120              TargetId = StableId(target),
0121              Purpose = purpose,
0122              Notes = "模拟案例显式指定，不依赖名称或时间自动配对。"
0123          });
0124      }
0125  }

0026      {
0027          return new AcousticProject();
0028      }
0029
0030      public string SourceSummary()
0031      {
0032          if (Trials.Count == 0)
0033          {
0034              return "尚无试验数据";
0035          }
0036
0037          return string.Join(" · ", Trials.GroupBy(trial => trial.Source)
0038              .OrderBy(group => group.Key)
0039              .Select(group => $"{group.Key.Text()} {group.Count()}"));
0040      }
0041
0042      public string LocationNames(IEnumerable<Guid> identifiers)
0043      {
0044          var map = Locations.ToDictionary(location => location.Id);
0045          return string.Join(" + ", identifiers.Select(id =>
0046              map.TryGetValue(id, out var location) ? location.Name : "无效位置"));
0047      }
0048
0049      public string ConditionName(Guid id)
0050      {
0051          return Conditions.FirstOrDefault(condition => condition.Id == id)?.Name ?? "未找到工况";
0052      }
0053
0054      public string TrialName(Guid id)
0055      {
0056          return Trials.FirstOrDefault(trial => trial.Id == id)?.Name ?? "未找到试验";
0057      }
0058  }
