================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Domain/Validation.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002
0003  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004
0005  public sealed record ValidationMessage(string Path, string Message)
0006  {
0007      public override string ToString()
0008      {
0009          return Path + "：" + Message;
0010      }
0011  }
0012
0013  public sealed class DomainValidationException : Exception
0014  {
0015      public IReadOnlyList<ValidationMessage> Problems { get; }
0016
0017      public DomainValidationException(string path, string message)
0018          : this([new ValidationMessage(path, message)])
0019      {
0020      }
0021
0022      public DomainValidationException(IReadOnlyList<ValidationMessage> problems)
0023          : base(string.Join("；", problems.Select(problem => problem.ToString())))
0024      {
0025          Problems = problems;
0026      }
0027  }
0028
0029  public sealed class ProjectValidator
0030  {
0031      public IReadOnlyList<ValidationMessage> Inspect(AcousticProject project)
0032      {
0033          var errors = new List<ValidationMessage>();
0034          if (project.SchemaVersion != Software.SchemaVersion)
0035          {
0036              errors.Add(new("schemaVersion", "格式版本不受支持，请保留原文件"));
0037          }
0038
0039          CheckId(project.Id, "project.id", errors);
0040          CheckText(project.Name, "project.name", 160, true, errors);
0041          CheckText(project.Owner, "project.owner", 120, false, errors);
0042          CheckText(project.VehicleProgramme, "project.vehicleProgramme", 200, false, errors);
0043          CheckText(project.Objective, "project.objective", 4000, false, errors);
0044          CheckText(project.Description, "project.description", 8000, false, errors);
0045          CheckText(project.SoftwareVersion, "softwareVersion", 40, true, errors);
0046          CheckEnum(project.Status, "project.status", errors);
0047          if (project.Revision < 0)
0048          {
0049              errors.Add(new("revision", "修订号不得为负"));
0050          }
0051
0052          CheckDate(project.CreatedAt, "createdAt", errors);
0053          CheckDate(project.UpdatedAt, "updatedAt", errors);
0054          if (project.Locations is null || project.Conditions is null || project.Trials is null ||
0055              project.Pairs is null || project.Settings is null || project.Audit is null || project.Reviews is null)
0056          {
0057              errors.Add(new("project", "对象集合、设置、审计或复核不得为null"));
0058              return errors;
0059          }
0060
0061          CheckCount(project.Locations.Count, 200, "locations", errors);
0062          CheckCount(project.Conditions.Count, 500, "conditions", errors);
0063          CheckCount(project.Trials.Count, Software.MaximumTrials, "trials", errors);
0064          CheckCount(project.Pairs.Count, Software.MaximumPairs, "pairs", errors);
0065          CheckCount(project.Audit.Count, 10000, "audit", errors);
0066          CheckCount(project.Reviews.Count, 2000, "reviews", errors);
0067          if (project.Locations.Any(item => item is null) || project.Conditions.Any(item => item is null) ||
0068              project.Trials.Any(item => item is null) || project.Pairs.Any(item => item is null) ||
0069              project.Audit.Any(item => item is null) || project.Reviews.Any(item => item is null))
0070          {
0071              errors.Add(new("project", "集合中不可包含null对象"));
0072              return errors;
0073          }
0074
0075          CheckUnique(project.Locations.Select(item => item.Id), "locations", errors);
0076          CheckUnique(project.Conditions.Select(item => item.Id), "conditions", errors);
0077          CheckUnique(project.Trials.Select(item => item.Id), "trials", errors);
0078          CheckUnique(project.Pairs.Select(item => item.Id), "pairs", errors);
0079          CheckUnique(project.Audit.Select(item => item.Id), "audit", errors);
0080          CheckUnique(project.Reviews.Select(item => item.Id), "reviews", errors);
0081          foreach (var location in project.Locations)
0082          {
0083              ValidateLocation(location, errors);
0084          }
0085
0086          foreach (var condition in project.Conditions)
0087          {
0088              ValidateCondition(condition, errors);
0089          }
0090
0091          var locationIds = project.Locations.Select(item => item.Id).ToHashSet();
0092          var conditionIds = project.Conditions.Select(item => item.Id).ToHashSet();
0093          foreach (var trial in project.Trials)
0094          {
0095              ValidateTrial(trial, locationIds, conditionIds, errors);
0096          }
0097
0098          var trialMap = project.Trials.GroupBy(item => item.Id)
0099              .ToDictionary(group => group.Key, group => group.First());
0100          foreach (var pair in project.Pairs)
0101          {
0102              ValidatePair(pair, trialMap, errors);
0103          }
0104
0105          var duplicatePair = project.Pairs.GroupBy(pair => (pair.BaselineId, pair.TargetId, pair.Purpose))
0106              .FirstOrDefault(group => group.Count() > 1);
0107          if (duplicatePair is not null)
0108          {
0109              errors.Add(new("pairs", "同一用途的相同基线和目标不可重复"));
0110          }
0111
0112          foreach (var setting in project.Settings.Describe())
0113          {
0114              CheckNumber(setting.Value, setting.Minimum, setting.Maximum, "settings." + setting.Key, false, errors);
0115          }
0116
0117          foreach (var entry in project.Audit)
0118          {
0119              CheckId(entry.Id, "audit.id", errors);
0120              CheckDate(entry.Time, "audit.time", errors);
0121              CheckText(entry.Action, "audit.action", 120, true, errors);
0122              CheckText(entry.Entity, "audit.entity", 200, false, errors);
0123              CheckText(entry.Detail, "audit.detail", 16000, false, errors);
0124              CheckText(entry.InputHash, "audit.inputHash", 64, false, errors);
0125              if (entry.Revision < 0 || entry.Revision > project.Revision)
0126              {
0127                  errors.Add(new("audit.revision", "审计修订号超出项目范围"));
0128              }
0129          }
0130
0131          foreach (var review in project.Reviews)
0132          {
0133              CheckId(review.Id, "reviews.id", errors);
0134              CheckDate(review.Time, "reviews.time", errors);
0135              CheckText(review.Reviewer, "reviews.reviewer", 120, true, errors);
0136              CheckText(review.Comment, "reviews.comment", 4000, false, errors);
0137              CheckText(review.SettingsJson, "reviews.settingsJson", 8000, true, errors);
0138              CheckText(review.InputHash, "reviews.inputHash", 64, true, errors);
0139              if (review.BlockingReasons is null)
0140              {
0141                  errors.Add(new("reviews.blockingReasons", "复核阻断原因列表不得为null"));
0142              }
0143              else
0144              {
0145                  CheckCount(review.BlockingReasons.Count, 5000, "reviews.blockingReasons", errors);
0146                  foreach (var reason in review.BlockingReasons)
0147                  {
0148                      CheckText(reason, "reviews.blockingReasons", 2000, true, errors);
0149                  }
0150              }
0151          }
0152
0153          return errors;
0154      }
0155
0156      public void Ensure(AcousticProject project)
0157      {
0158          var errors = Inspect(project);
0159          if (errors.Count != 0)
0160          {
0161              throw new DomainValidationException(errors);
0162          }
0163      }
0164
0165      private static void ValidateLocation(SealLocation location, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0166      {
0167          var path = "locations[" + location.Id + "]";
0168          CheckText(location.Name, path + ".name", 120, true, errors);
0169          CheckText(location.Zone, path + ".zone", 120, false, errors);
0170          CheckText(location.Material, path + ".material", 1000, false, errors);
0171          CheckText(location.Extent, path + ".extent", 1000, false, errors);
0172          CheckText(location.Procedure, path + ".procedure", 4000, false, errors);
0173          CheckText(location.RecoveryProcedure, path + ".recoveryProcedure", 4000, false, errors);
0174          CheckText(location.Reference, path + ".reference", 1000, false, errors);
0175          CheckText(location.Notes, path + ".notes", 4000, false, errors);
0176      }
0177
0178      private static void ValidateCondition(MeasurementCondition condition, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0179      {
0180          var path = "conditions[" + condition.Id + "]";
0181          CheckText(condition.Name, path + ".name", 120, true, errors);
0182          foreach (var field in condition.TextFields())
0183          {
0184              CheckText(field.Value, path + "." + field.Key, 500, false, errors);
0185          }
0186
0187          CheckNumber(condition.Speed, 0, 500, path + ".speed", true, errors);
0188          CheckNumber(condition.Rpm, 0, 30000, path + ".rpm", true, errors);
0189          CheckNumber(condition.Temperature, -80, 100, path + ".temperature", true, errors);
0190          CheckNumber(condition.Duration, 0.001, 86400, path + ".duration", true, errors);
0191          CheckNumber(condition.SampleRate, 1, 1000000, path + ".sampleRate", true, errors);
0192          CheckText(condition.Synchronization, path + ".synchronization", 500, false, errors);
0193          CheckText(condition.Notes, path + ".notes", 4000, false, errors);
0194          if (condition.Weighting is not ("" or "A" or "C" or "Z"))
0195          {
0196              errors.Add(new(path + ".weighting", "频率计权仅支持A、C、Z或草稿空白"));
0197          }
0198
0199          if (condition.BandType is not ("" or "octave" or "third-octave" or "custom"))
0200          {
0201              errors.Add(new(path + ".bandType", "频带制须为octave、third-octave或custom"));
0202          }
0203
0204          if (condition.Unit is not ("" or Software.LevelUnit))
0205          {
0206              errors.Add(new(path + ".unit", "仅支持" + Software.LevelUnit));
0207          }
0208      }
0209
0210      private static void ValidateTrial(
0211          AcousticTrial trial,
0212          HashSet<Guid> locations,
0213          HashSet<Guid> conditions,
0214          List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0215      {
0216          var path = "trials[" + trial.Id + "]";
0217          CheckText(trial.Name, path + ".name", 160, true, errors);
0218          CheckEnum(trial.Kind, path + ".kind", errors);
0219          CheckEnum(trial.Source, path + ".source", errors);
0220          CheckDate(trial.Timestamp, path + ".timestamp", errors);
0221          CheckText(trial.RepeatGroup, path + ".repeatGroup", 120, false, errors);
0222          CheckText(trial.SourceNote, path + ".sourceNote", 4000, true, errors);
0223          CheckText(trial.OriginalFile, path + ".originalFile", 1000, false, errors);
0224          CheckText(trial.Operator, path + ".operator", 120, false, errors);
0225          CheckText(trial.Preparation, path + ".preparation", 4000, false, errors);
0226          CheckText(trial.Notes, path + ".notes", 4000, false, errors);
0227          if (!conditions.Contains(trial.ConditionId))
0228          {
0229              errors.Add(new(path + ".conditionId", "引用的工况不存在"));
0230          }
0231
0232          if (trial.LocationIds is null || trial.Bands is null)
0233          {
0234              errors.Add(new(path, "封堵位置集合与频带集合不得为null"));
0235              return;
0236          }
0237
0238          if (trial.LocationIds.Distinct().Count() != trial.LocationIds.Count)
0239          {
0240              errors.Add(new(path + ".locationIds", "封堵位置不可重复"));
0241          }
0242
0243          if (trial.LocationIds.Any(id => !locations.Contains(id)))
0244          {
0245              errors.Add(new(path + ".locationIds", "引用的封堵位置不存在"));
0246          }
0247
0248          var count = trial.LocationIds.Count;
0249          if (trial.Kind is TrialKind.Baseline or TrialKind.Recovery && count != 0)
0250          {
0251              errors.Add(new(path + ".locationIds", "基线和恢复不得选择封堵位置"));
0252          }
0253
0254          if (trial.Kind == TrialKind.Single && count != 1)
0255          {
0256              errors.Add(new(path + ".locationIds", "单项封堵须选择一个位置"));
0257          }
0258
0259          if (trial.Kind == TrialKind.Combination && count < 2)
0260          {
0261              errors.Add(new(path + ".locationIds", "组合封堵至少选择两个位置"));
0262          }
0263
0264          CheckCount(trial.Bands.Count, Software.MaximumBands, path + ".bands", errors);
0265          if (trial.Bands.Any(band => band is null))
0266          {
0267              errors.Add(new(path + ".bands", "频带不可为null对象"));
0268              return;
0269          }
0270
0271          foreach (var band in trial.Bands)
0272          {
0273              var bandPath = path + ".bands[" + band.Frequency.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "]";
0274              CheckNumber(band.Frequency, 1, 100000, bandPath + ".frequency", false, errors);
0275              CheckNumber(band.Level, -100, 200, bandPath + ".level", true, errors);
0276              CheckText(band.Note, bandPath + ".note", 1000, false, errors);
0277          }
0278
0279          if (trial.Bands.GroupBy(band => band.Frequency).Any(group => group.Count() > 1))
0280          {
0281              errors.Add(new(path + ".bands", "同一试验频率不可重复"));
0282          }
0283      }
0284
0285      private static void ValidatePair(TrialPair pair, Dictionary<Guid, AcousticTrial> trials, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0286      {
0287          var path = "pairs[" + pair.Id + "]";
0288          CheckText(pair.Notes, path + ".notes", 4000, false, errors);
0289          CheckEnum(pair.Purpose, path + ".purpose", errors);
0290          if (!trials.TryGetValue(pair.BaselineId, out var baseline) ||
0291              !trials.TryGetValue(pair.TargetId, out var target))
0292          {
0293              errors.Add(new(path, "配对引用的试验不存在"));
0294              return;
0295          }
0296
0297          if (baseline.Id == target.Id)
0298          {
0299              errors.Add(new(path, "不能与自身配对"));
0300          }
0301
0302          if (baseline.Kind != TrialKind.Baseline)
0303          {
0304              errors.Add(new(path + ".baselineId", "基准须为基线试验"));
0305          }
0306
0307          var allowed = pair.Purpose switch
0308          {
0309              PairPurpose.Effect => target.Kind is TrialKind.Single or TrialKind.Combination,
0310              PairPurpose.Drift => target.Kind == TrialKind.Baseline,
0311              PairPurpose.Recovery => target.Kind == TrialKind.Recovery,
0312              _ => false
0313          };
0314          if (!allowed)
0315          {
0316              errors.Add(new(path + ".targetId", "目标试验类型与配对用途不匹配"));
0317          }
0318      }
0319
0320      private static void CheckText(string? value, string path, int maximum, bool required, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0321      {
0322          if (value is null)
0323          {
0324              errors.Add(new(path, "文本不得为null"));
0325              return;
0326          }
0327
0328          if (required && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
0329          {
0330              errors.Add(new(path, "不能为空"));
0331          }
0332
0333          if (value.Length > maximum)
0334          {
0335              errors.Add(new(path, $"最多{maximum}字符"));
0336          }
0337
0338          if (value.Any(character => char.IsControl(character) && character is not ('\r' or '\n' or '\t')))
0339          {
0340              errors.Add(new(path, "包含不可用控制字符"));
0341          }
0342      }
0343
0344      private static void CheckNumber(double? value, double minimum, double maximum, string path, bool nullable, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0345      {
0346          if (!value.HasValue)
0347          {
0348              if (!nullable)
0349              {
0350                  errors.Add(new(path, "数值不能为空"));
0351              }
0352
0353              return;
0354          }
0355
0356          if (!double.IsFinite(value.Value) || value < minimum || value > maximum)
0357          {
0358              errors.Add(new(path, $"须为{minimum}至{maximum}的有限数值"));
0359          }
0360      }
0361
0362      private static void CheckId(Guid id, string path, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0363      {
0364          if (id == Guid.Empty)
0365          {
0366              errors.Add(new(path, "对象标识不可为空"));
0367          }
0368      }
0369
0370      private static void CheckUnique(IEnumerable<Guid> ids, string path, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0371      {
0372          var values = ids.ToList();
0373          if (values.Contains(Guid.Empty) || values.Distinct().Count() != values.Count)
0374          {
0375              errors.Add(new(path, "对象标识为空或重复"));
0376          }
0377      }
0378
0379      private static void CheckDate(DateTimeOffset date, string path, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0380      {
0381          if (date.Year < 1900 || date.Year > 2200)
0382          {
0383              errors.Add(new(path, "日期范围须为1900至2200年，保留时区"));
0384          }
0385      }
0386
0387      private static void CheckCount(int count, int maximum, string path, List<ValidationMessage> errors)
0388      {
0389          if (count > maximum)
0390          {
0391              errors.Add(new(path, $"最多允许{maximum}项"));
0392          }
0393      }
0394
0395      private static void CheckEnum<T>(T value, string path, List<ValidationMessage> errors) where T : struct, Enum
0396      {
0397          if (!Enum.IsDefined(value))
0398          {
0399              errors.Add(new(path, "枚举值无效"));
0400          }
0401      }
0402  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/IO/CsvImport.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Text;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004  using AcousticSeal.Core.Operations;
0005
0006  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0007
0008  public sealed record CsvRecord(int Line, List<string> Cells);
0009  public sealed record ImportChange(Guid TrialId, string TrialName, int Added, int Replaced, int Missing, List<BandMeasurement> Bands);
0010
0011  public sealed class ImportPreview
0012  {
0013      public string ProjectHash { get; init; } = "";
0014      public ImportMode Mode { get; init; }
0015      public char Delimiter { get; init; }
0016      public int Rows { get; init; }
0017      public List<ValidationMessage> Problems { get; init; } = [];
0018      public List<ImportChange> Changes { get; init; } = [];
0019      public bool CanApply => Problems.Count == 0 && Rows > 0 && Changes.Count > 0;
0020      public int Missing => Changes.Sum(change => change.Missing);
0021      public int Replaced => Changes.Sum(change => change.Replaced);
0022  }
0023
0024  public sealed class CsvImportService
0025  {
0026      public ImportPreview Preview(AcousticProject project, string content, ImportMode mode, char delimiter = ',')
0027      {
0028          if (delimiter is not (',' or ';' or '\t'))
0029          {
0030              throw new DomainValidationException("delimiter", "仅支持逗号、分号或制表符");
0031          }
0032
0033          if (Encoding.UTF8.GetByteCount(content) > 5 * 1024 * 1024)
0034          {
0035              throw new DomainValidationException("csv", "CSV不得超过5MiB");
0036          }
0037
0038          var records = CsvParser.Read(content.TrimStart('\uFEFF'), delimiter);
0039          var problems = new List<ValidationMessage>();
0040          var expected = new[] { "trial_id", "frequency_hz", "level_db" };
0041          if (records.Count == 0 || !records[0].Cells.SequenceEqual(expected))
0042          {
0043              throw new DomainValidationException("csv.header", "表头必须为trial_id、frequency_hz、level_db三列，分隔符须与选择一致");
0044          }
0045
0046          if (records.Count > 100001)
0047          {
0048              throw new DomainValidationException("csv", "最多允许100000数据行");
0049          }
0050
0051          var trials = project.Trials.ToDictionary(trial => trial.Id);
0052          var updates = new Dictionary<Guid, List<BandMeasurement>>();
0053          var seen = new HashSet<(Guid, double)>();
0054          foreach (var record in records.Skip(1))
0055          {
0056              var path = "CSV第" + record.Line + "行";
0057              if (record.Cells.Count != 3)
0058              {
0059                  problems.Add(new(path, "列数不是3"));
0060                  continue;
0061              }
0062
0063              if (!Guid.TryParse(record.Cells[0].Trim(), out var id) || !trials.ContainsKey(id))
0064              {
0065                  problems.Add(new(path + ".trial_id", "试验标识不存在；先建立试验并使用其GUID"));
0066                  continue;
0067              }
0068
0069              if (!double.TryParse(record.Cells[1].Trim(), NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var frequency) ||
0070                  !double.IsFinite(frequency) || frequency < 1 || frequency > 100000)
0071              {
0072                  problems.Add(new(path + ".frequency_hz", "频率须为1至100000Hz有限数值"));
0073                  continue;
0074              }
0075
0076              if (!seen.Add((id, frequency)))
0077              {
0078                  problems.Add(new(path + ".frequency_hz", "同一试验频率重复，整批不可应用"));
0079                  continue;
0080              }
0081
0082              var text = record.Cells[2].Trim();
0083              double? level = null;
0084              if (text is not ("" or "NA" or "N/A" or "null"))
0085              {
0086                  if (!double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var value) ||
0087                      !double.IsFinite(value) || value < -100 || value > 200)
0088                  {
0089                      problems.Add(new(path + ".level_db", "声级须为-100至200dB，或空白/NA/N/A/null"));
0090                      continue;
0091                  }
0092
0093                  level = value;
0094              }
0095
0096              if (!updates.TryGetValue(id, out var bands))
0097              {
0098                  bands = [];
0099                  updates[id] = bands;
0100              }
0101
0102              bands.Add(new BandMeasurement { Frequency = frequency, Level = level });
0103          }
0104
0105          var changes = new List<ImportChange>();
0106          foreach (var (id, bands) in updates)
0107          {
0108              var trial = trials[id];
0109              var original = trial.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency).ToHashSet();
0110              var resultCount = mode == ImportMode.Replace ? bands.Count : original.Union(bands.Select(band => band.Frequency)).Count();
0111              if (resultCount > Software.MaximumBands)
0112              {
0113                  problems.Add(new("csv." + trial.Name, "应用后超过每试验200频带"));
0114              }
0115
0116              changes.Add(new ImportChange(id, trial.Name, bands.Count(band => !original.Contains(band.Frequency)),
0117                  bands.Count(band => original.Contains(band.Frequency)), bands.Count(band => !band.Level.HasValue), bands));
0118          }
0119
0120          if (records.Count == 1)
0121          {
0122              problems.Add(new("csv", "没有数据行"));
0123          }
0124
0125          return new ImportPreview
0126          {
0127              ProjectHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project),
0128              Mode = mode,
0129              Delimiter = delimiter,
0130              Rows = records.Count - 1,
0131              Problems = problems,
0132              Changes = changes
0133          };
0134      }
0135
0136      public void Apply(ProjectSession session, ImportPreview preview)
0137      {
0138          if (!preview.CanApply)
0139          {
0140              throw new DomainValidationException("csv", "预检未通过，数据没有改变");
0141          }
0142
0143          if (ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(session.Project) != preview.ProjectHash)
0144          {
0145              throw new DomainValidationException("csv", "预检后项目已改变，请重新预检，禁止应用过期结果");
0146          }
0147
0148          session.Apply("CSV整体导入：" + preview.Mode, session.Project.Id.ToString(), project =>
0149          {
0150              foreach (var change in preview.Changes)
0151              {
0152                  var trial = project.Trials.Single(trial => trial.Id == change.TrialId);
0153                  if (preview.Mode == ImportMode.Replace)
0154                  {
0155                      trial.Bands = ProjectCodec.Clone(change.Bands);
0156                  }
0157                  else
0158                  {
0159                      var updated = change.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency).ToHashSet();
0160                      trial.Bands.RemoveAll(band => updated.Contains(band.Frequency));
0161                      trial.Bands.AddRange(ProjectCodec.Clone(change.Bands));
0162                  }
0163
0164                  trial.Bands = trial.Bands.OrderBy(band => band.Frequency).ToList();
0165              }
0166          });
0167      }
0168
0169      public static string DecodeUtf8(byte[] bytes)
0170      {
0171          try
0172          {
0173              return new UTF8Encoding(false, true).GetString(bytes).TrimStart('\uFEFF');
0174          }
0175          catch (DecoderFallbackException)
0176          {
0177              throw new DomainValidationException("csv.encoding", "不是有效UTF-8，请另存为CSV UTF-8");
0178          }
0179      }
0180  }
0181
0182  public static class CsvParser
0183  {
0184      public static List<CsvRecord> Read(string content, char delimiter)
0185      {
0186          var records = new List<CsvRecord>();
0187          var cells = new List<string>();
0188          var cell = new StringBuilder();
0189          var quoted = false;
0190          var closedQuote = false;
0191          var line = 1;
0192          var startLine = 1;
0193          for (var index = 0; index < content.Length; index++)
0194          {
0195              var character = content[index];
0196              if (quoted)
0197              {
0198                  if (character == '"' && index + 1 < content.Length && content[index + 1] == '"')
0199                  {
0200                      cell.Append('"');
0201                      index++;
0202                  }
0203                  else if (character == '"')
0204                  {
0205                      quoted = false;
0206                      closedQuote = true;
0207                  }
0208                  else
0209                  {
0210                      cell.Append(character);
0211                      if (character == '\n')
0212                      {
0213                          line++;
0214                      }
0215                  }
0216
0217                  continue;
0218              }
0219
0220              if (character == delimiter)
0221              {
0222                  cells.Add(cell.ToString());
0223                  cell.Clear();
0224                  closedQuote = false;
0225              }
0226              else if (character is '\r' or '\n')
0227              {
0228                  if (character == '\r' && index + 1 < content.Length && content[index + 1] == '\n')
0229                  {
0230                      index++;
0231                  }
0232
0233                  cells.Add(cell.ToString());
0234                  if (cells.Any(value => value.Length > 0))
0235                  {
0236                      records.Add(new CsvRecord(startLine, cells));
0237                  }
0238
0239                  cells = [];
0240                  cell.Clear();
0241                  closedQuote = false;
0242                  line++;
0243                  startLine = line;
0244              }
0245              else if (character == '"' && cell.Length == 0 && !closedQuote)
0246              {
0247                  quoted = true;
0248              }
0249              else if (closedQuote || character == '"')
0250              {
0251                  throw new DomainValidationException("CSV第" + line + "行", "引号位置错误或闭引号后仍有内容");
0252              }
0253              else
0254              {
0255                  cell.Append(character);
0256              }
0257          }
0258
0259          if (quoted)
0260          {
0261              throw new DomainValidationException("CSV第" + startLine + "行", "引号未闭合");
0262          }
0263
0264          if (cell.Length > 0 || cells.Count > 0 || closedQuote)
0265          {
0266              cells.Add(cell.ToString());
0267              records.Add(new CsvRecord(startLine, cells));
0268          }
0269
0270          return records;
0271      }
0272  }

src/AcousticSeal.Core/IO/LegacyMigration.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Security.Cryptography;
0003  using System.Text;
0004  using System.Text.Json;
0005  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0006
0007  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0008
0009  public static class LegacyMigration
0010  {
0011      public static DecodedProject Convert(JsonElement root)
0012      {
0013          var project = new AcousticProject
0014          {
0015              Id = Id(Text(root, "id")),
0016              Name = Text(root, "name"),
0017              Owner = Text(root, "owner"),
0018              Description = Text(root, "description"),
0019              CreatedAt = Date(root, "createdAt"),
0020              UpdatedAt = Date(root, "updatedAt"),
0021              Revision = root.GetProperty("revision").GetInt64(),
0022              Status = Text(root, "status") == "archived" ? ProjectStatus.Archived : ProjectStatus.Active
0023          };
0024          foreach (var site in Array(root, "sites"))
0025          {
0026              project.Locations.Add(new SealLocation
0027              {
0028                  Id = Id(Text(site, "id")),
0029                  Name = Text(site, "name"),
0030                  Zone = Text(site, "zone"),
0031                  Material = Text(site, "material"),
0032                  Procedure = Text(site, "procedure"),
0033                  Notes = Text(site, "notes")
0034              });
0035          }
0036
0037          foreach (var condition in Array(root, "conditions"))
0038          {
0039              project.Conditions.Add(new MeasurementCondition
0040              {
0041                  Id = Id(Text(condition, "id")),
0042                  Name = Text(condition, "name"),
0043                  Vehicle = Text(condition, "vehicle"),
0044                  Microphone = Text(condition, "microphone"),
0045                  Excitation = Text(condition, "excitation"),
0046                  Surface = Text(condition, "surface"),
0047                  Gear = Text(condition, "gear"),
0048                  Weighting = Text(condition, "weighting"),
0049                  TimeDefinition = Text(condition, "timeWeighting"),
0050                  BandType = Text(condition, "bandType"),
0051                  Instrument = Text(condition, "instrument"),
0052                  Calibration = Text(condition, "calibration"),
0053                  Unit = Text(condition, "unit"),
0054                  Speed = Number(condition, "speed"),
0055                  Rpm = Number(condition, "rpm"),
0056                  Temperature = Number(condition, "temperature"),
0057                  Duration = Number(condition, "duration"),
0058                  NonOverlappingBands = condition.GetProperty("independentBands").GetBoolean(),
0059                  Notes = Text(condition, "notes")
0060              });
0061          }
0062
0063          foreach (var trial in Array(root, "trials"))
0064          {
0065              var kind = Text(trial, "type") switch
0066              {
0067                  "baseline" => TrialKind.Baseline,
0068                  "single" => TrialKind.Single,
0069                  "combination" => TrialKind.Combination,
0070                  "recovery" => TrialKind.Recovery,
0071                  _ => throw new DomainValidationException("legacy.type", "旧试验类型无效")
0072              };
0073              var source = Text(trial, "sourceKind") switch
0074              {
0075                  "simulation" => SourceKind.Simulation,
0076                  "measured" => SourceKind.Measured,
0077                  "manual" => SourceKind.Manual,
0078                  _ => throw new DomainValidationException("legacy.sourceKind", "旧来源类别无效")
0079              };
0080              var bands = new List<BandMeasurement>();
0081              foreach (var band in trial.GetProperty("bands").EnumerateObject())
0082              {
0083                  if (!double.TryParse(band.Name, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var frequency))
0084                  {
0085                      throw new DomainValidationException("legacy.bands", "频率不是数值");
0086                  }
0087
0088                  bands.Add(new BandMeasurement
0089                  {
0090                      Frequency = frequency,
0091                      Level = band.Value.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Null ? null : band.Value.GetDouble()
0092                  });
0093              }
0094
0095              project.Trials.Add(new AcousticTrial
0096              {
0097                  Id = Id(Text(trial, "id")),
0098                  Name = Text(trial, "name"),
0099                  Kind = kind,
0100                  ConditionId = Id(Text(trial, "conditionId")),
0101                  LocationIds = Array(trial, "siteIds").Select(item => Id(item.GetString()!)).ToList(),
0102                  Timestamp = Date(trial, "timestamp"),
0103                  RepeatGroup = Text(trial, "repeatGroup"),
0104                  Source = source,
0105                  SourceNote = Text(trial, "sourceNote"),
0106                  Operator = Text(trial, "operator"),
0107                  Notes = Text(trial, "notes"),
0108                  Bands = bands
0109              });
0110          }
0111
0112          foreach (var pair in Array(root, "pairs"))
0113          {
0114              var purpose = Text(pair, "purpose") switch
0115              {
0116                  "effect" => PairPurpose.Effect,
0117                  "drift" => PairPurpose.Drift,
0118                  "recovery" => PairPurpose.Recovery,
0119                  _ => throw new DomainValidationException("legacy.purpose", "旧配对用途无效")
0120              };
0121              project.Pairs.Add(new TrialPair
0122              {
0123                  Id = Id(Text(pair, "id")),
0124                  BaselineId = Id(Text(pair, "baselineId")),
0125                  TargetId = Id(Text(pair, "targetId")),
0126                  Purpose = purpose,
0127                  Notes = Text(pair, "notes")
0128              });
0129          }
0130
0131          var parameters = root.GetProperty("parameters");
0132          project.Settings.SpeedTolerance = Number(parameters, "speedTolerance") ?? 0.5;
0133          project.Settings.RpmTolerance = Number(parameters, "rpmTolerance") ?? 20;
0134          project.Settings.TemperatureTolerance = Number(parameters, "temperatureTolerance") ?? 2;
0135          project.Settings.DurationTolerance = Number(parameters, "durationTolerance") ?? 0.01;
0136          project.Settings.DriftThreshold = Number(parameters, "driftThreshold") ?? 1;
0137          project.Settings.RecoveryThreshold = Number(parameters, "recoveryThreshold") ?? 1;
0138          project.Settings.RepeatabilityThreshold = Number(parameters, "repeatabilityThreshold") ?? 1;
0139          foreach (var entry in Array(root, "audit"))
0140          {
0141              project.Audit.Add(new AuditEntry
0142              {
0143                  Time = Date(entry, "time"),
0144                  Action = Text(entry, "action"),
0145                  Detail = Text(entry, "detail"),
0146                  Entity = "legacy",
0147                  Revision = 0
0148              });
0149          }
0150
0151          project.Revision++;
0152          project.UpdatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
0153          project.Audit.Add(new AuditEntry
0154          {
0155              Action = "导入旧格式v1",
0156              Entity = project.Id.ToString(),
0157              Revision = project.Revision,
0158              Detail = "将旧字符串标识确定性映射为GUID；保留试验来源和频带；旧文件未修改"
0159          });
0160          return new DecodedProject(project, true,
0161          [
0162              "检测到旧版.aslp.json，已在内存中迁移至schema2；原文件不变，请另存.sealproj。",
0163              "旧字符串标识映射为稳定GUID，原位置、工况、试验、配对和来源保留。",
0164              "新增采样率、同步定义、准备与恢复工艺字段留空，不捏造缺少的记录。"
0165          ]);
0166      }
0167
0168      public static Guid Id(string value)
0169      {
0170          if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
0171          {
0172              throw new DomainValidationException("legacy.id", "旧标识不能为空");
0173          }
0174
0175          return Guid.TryParse(value, out var guid) ? guid : new Guid(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("AcousticSeal/v1/" + value))[..16]);
0176      }
0177
0178      private static string Text(JsonElement element, string name)
0179      {
0180          return element.TryGetProperty(name, out var value) && value.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.String
0181              ? value.GetString()! : "";
0182      }
0183
0184      private static double? Number(JsonElement element, string name)
0185      {
0186          return element.TryGetProperty(name, out var value) && value.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Null
0187              ? value.GetDouble() : null;
0188      }
0189
0190      private static IEnumerable<JsonElement> Array(JsonElement element, string name)
0191      {
0192          if (!element.TryGetProperty(name, out var value) || value.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array)
0193          {
0194              throw new DomainValidationException("legacy." + name, "旧文件缺少必要集合");
0195          }
0196
0197          return value.EnumerateArray();
0198      }
0199
0200      private static DateTimeOffset Date(JsonElement element, string name)
0201      {
0202          var text = Text(element, name);
0203          if (text.Length < 6 || !(text.EndsWith('Z') || text[^6] is '+' or '-') ||
0204              !DateTimeOffset.TryParse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var value))
0205          {
0206              throw new DomainValidationException("legacy." + name, "旧时间格式无效或缺少时区");
0207          }
0208
0209          return value;
0210      }
0211  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/IO/ProjectCodec.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Security.Cryptography;
0002  using System.Text;
0003  using System.Text.Json;
0004  using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
0005  using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
0006  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0007
0008  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0009
0010  public sealed record DecodedProject(AcousticProject Project, bool Migrated, List<string> Notes);
0011
0012  public sealed class ProjectCodec
0013  {
0014      public static readonly JsonSerializerOptions Options = CreateOptions();
0015      private readonly ProjectValidator validator = new();
0016
0017      public byte[] Encode(AcousticProject project)
0018      {
0019          validator.Ensure(project);
0020          return JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(project, Options);
0021      }
0022
0023      public DecodedProject Decode(byte[] bytes)
0024      {
0025          if (bytes.Length == 0 || bytes.Length > Software.MaximumFileBytes)
0026          {
0027              throw new DomainValidationException("file", "项目文件须为1字节至12MiB");
0028          }
0029
0030          string content;
0031          try
0032          {
0033              content = new UTF8Encoding(false, true).GetString(bytes).TrimStart('\uFEFF');
0034          }
0035          catch (DecoderFallbackException)
0036          {
0037              throw new DomainValidationException("file.encoding", "文件不是有效UTF-8，请保留原文件");
0038          }
0039
0040          try
0041          {
0042              using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(content, new JsonDocumentOptions { MaxDepth = 64 });
0043              InspectDuplicates(document.RootElement, "project");
0044              if (document.RootElement.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Object ||
0045                  !document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("schemaVersion", out var version) ||
0046                  version.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Number || !version.TryGetInt32(out var number))
0047              {
0048                  throw new DomainValidationException("schemaVersion", "缺少整数格式版本");
0049              }
0050
0051              if (number == 1)
0052              {
0053                  var migrated = LegacyMigration.Convert(document.RootElement);
0054                  validator.Ensure(migrated.Project);
0055                  return migrated;
0056              }
0057
0058              if (number != Software.SchemaVersion)
0059              {
0060                  throw new DomainValidationException("schemaVersion", "不支持此版本，不修改原文件");
0061              }
0062
0063              foreach (var required in new[] { "id", "name", "createdAt", "updatedAt", "revision", "status", "locations", "conditions", "trials", "pairs", "settings", "audit", "reviews" })
0064              {
0065                  if (!document.RootElement.TryGetProperty(required, out _))
0066                  {
0067                      throw new DomainValidationException(required, "项目缺少必要字段");
0068                  }
0069              }
0070
0071              InspectRequiredObjects(document.RootElement);
0072              var project = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<AcousticProject>(content, Options)
0073                  ?? throw new DomainValidationException("project", "项目内容为空");
0074              validator.Ensure(project);
0075              return new DecodedProject(project, false, []);
0076          }
0077          catch (Exception error) when (error is InvalidOperationException or KeyNotFoundException or FormatException)
0078          {
0079              throw new DomainValidationException("file.structure", "项目字段缺失或类型不匹配：" + error.Message);
0080          }
0081          catch (JsonException error)
0082          {
0083              throw new DomainValidationException(error.Path ?? "json", "JSON格式或类型错误：" + error.Message);
0084          }
0085      }
0086
0087      public static T Clone<T>(T value)
0088      {
0089          var bytes = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(value, Options);
0090          return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<T>(bytes, Options)!;
0091      }
0092
0093      public static string Fingerprint(AcousticProject project)
0094      {
0095          var copy = Clone(project);
0096          copy.Audit = [];
0097          copy.Reviews = [];
0098          copy.Revision = 0;
0099          copy.UpdatedAt = copy.CreatedAt;
0100          return Convert.ToHexStringLower(SHA256.HashData(JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(copy, Options)));
0101      }
0102
0103      private static void InspectDuplicates(JsonElement element, string path)
0104      {
0105          if (element.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object)
0106          {
0107              var seen = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
0108              foreach (var property in element.EnumerateObject())
0109              {
0110                  if (!seen.Add(property.Name))
0111                  {
0112                      throw new DomainValidationException(path, "重复JSON字段：" + property.Name);
0113                  }
0114
0115                  InspectDuplicates(property.Value, path + "." + property.Name);
0116              }
0117          }
0118          else if (element.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array)
0119          {
0120              var index = 0;
0121              foreach (var item in element.EnumerateArray())
0122              {
0123                  InspectDuplicates(item, path + "[" + index++ + "]");
0124              }
0125          }
0126      }
0127
0128      private static void InspectRequiredObjects(JsonElement root)
0129      {
0130          var requirements = new Dictionary<string, string[]>
0131          {
0132              ["locations"] = ["id", "name"],
0133              ["conditions"] = ["id", "name"],
0134              ["trials"] = ["id", "name", "kind", "conditionId", "locationIds", "timestamp", "source", "sourceNote", "bands"],
0135              ["pairs"] = ["id", "baselineId", "targetId", "purpose"],
0136              ["audit"] = ["id", "time", "revision", "action", "entity", "detail", "inputHash"],
0137              ["reviews"] = ["id", "time", "inputRevision", "reviewer", "validPairs", "blockedPairs", "blockingReasons", "settingsJson", "inputHash"]
0138          };
0139          foreach (var (collection, names) in requirements)
0140          {
0141              var array = root.GetProperty(collection);
0142              if (array.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Array)
0143              {
0144                  throw new DomainValidationException(collection, "必要对象集合须为数组");
0145              }
0146
0147              var index = 0;
0148              foreach (var item in array.EnumerateArray())
0149              {
0150                  RequireProperties(item, names, collection + "[" + index++ + "]");
0151              }
0152          }
0153
0154          var settings = new AnalysisSettings().Describe().Select(setting => JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase.ConvertName(setting.Key)).ToList();
0155          settings.Add("requireSampleRateMatch");
0156          settings.Add("requireSynchronizationMatch");
0157          RequireProperties(root.GetProperty("settings"), settings, "settings");
0158      }
0159
0160      private static void RequireProperties(JsonElement element, IEnumerable<string> names, string path)
0161      {
0162          if (element.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Object)
0163          {
0164              throw new DomainValidationException(path, "对象缺失或类型无效");
0165          }
0166
0167          foreach (var name in names)
0168          {
0169              if (!element.TryGetProperty(name, out _))
0170              {
0171                  throw new DomainValidationException(path + "." + name, "缺少必要字段，不自动生成标识或猜测来源");
0172              }
0173          }
0174      }
0175
0176      private static JsonSerializerOptions CreateOptions()
0177      {
0178          var options = new JsonSerializerOptions
0179          {
0180              PropertyNamingPolicy = JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase,
0181              WriteIndented = true,
0182              MaxDepth = 64,
0183              UnmappedMemberHandling = JsonUnmappedMemberHandling.Disallow,
0184              Encoder = System.Text.Encodings.Web.JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
0185          };
0186          options.Converters.Add(new JsonStringEnumConverter(JsonNamingPolicy.CamelCase, allowIntegerValues: false));
0187          options.Converters.Add(new OffsetDateConverter());
0188          return options;
0189      }
0190  }
0191
0192  public sealed class OffsetDateConverter : JsonConverter<DateTimeOffset>
0193  {
0194      public override DateTimeOffset Read(ref Utf8JsonReader reader, Type type, JsonSerializerOptions options)
0195      {
0196          var text = reader.GetString();
0197          if (text is null || !Regex.IsMatch(text, "(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant) ||
0198              !DateTimeOffset.TryParse(text, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
0199                  System.Globalization.DateTimeStyles.None, out var value))
0200          {
0201              throw new JsonException("日期时间必须带时区，例如2026-01-01T10:00:00+08:00");
0202          }
0203
0204          return value;
0205      }
0206
0207      public override void Write(Utf8JsonWriter writer, DateTimeOffset value, JsonSerializerOptions options)
0208      {
0209          writer.WriteStringValue(value.ToString("O", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
0210      }
0211  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/IO/ProjectStore.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Security.Cryptography;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003
0004  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0005
0006  public sealed record StoredProject(AcousticProject Project, string ETag, string Path, bool FromBackup = false);
0007  public sealed record SaveReceipt(string Path, string BackupPath, string ETag, long Bytes);
0008  public sealed record ProjectCard(Guid Id, string Name, long Revision, DateTimeOffset Modified, string Path, bool HasBackup, string Error);
0009
0010  public sealed class ProjectConflictException : Exception
0011  {
0012      public ProjectConflictException()
0013          : base("磁盘项目已被其他会话修改或删除，已阻止覆盖。请另存副本，或重新打开磁盘版本后再编辑。")
0014      {
0015      }
0016  }
0017
0018  public sealed class ProjectStore
0019  {
0020      private readonly string directory;
0021      private readonly ProjectCodec codec = new();
0022      private readonly SemaphoreSlim gate = new(1, 1);
0023
0024      public ProjectStore(string directory)
0025      {
0026          this.directory = System.IO.Path.GetFullPath(directory);
0027          Directory.CreateDirectory(this.directory);
0028      }
0029
0030      public string DirectoryPath => directory;
0031
0032      public async Task<List<ProjectCard>> ListAsync(CancellationToken cancellation = default)
0033      {
0034          var cards = new List<ProjectCard>();
0035          foreach (var file in Directory.EnumerateFiles(directory, "*" + Software.Extension))
0036          {
0037              cancellation.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
0038              var filename = System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(file);
0039              if (!Guid.TryParseExact(filename, "N", out var id))
0040              {
0041                  continue;
0042              }
0043
0044              try
0045              {
0046                  var stored = await LoadAsync(id, cancellation);
0047                  cards.Add(new ProjectCard(id, stored.Project.Name, stored.Project.Revision,
0048                      stored.Project.UpdatedAt, file, File.Exists(file + ".bak"), ""));
0049              }
0050              catch (Exception error) when (error is IOException or DomainValidationException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
0051              {
0052                  cards.Add(new ProjectCard(id, "无法读取的项目", 0, new DateTimeOffset(File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(file)),
0053                      file, File.Exists(file + ".bak"), error.Message));
0054              }
0055          }
0056
0057          return cards.OrderByDescending(card => card.Modified).ToList();
0058      }
0059
0060      public async Task<StoredProject> LoadAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken cancellation = default)
0061      {
0062          await gate.WaitAsync(cancellation);
0063          try
0064          {
0065              var file = PathFor(id);
0066              var bytes = await ReadBoundedAsync(file, cancellation);
0067              var decoded = codec.Decode(bytes);
0068              if (decoded.Project.Id != id)
0069              {
0070                  throw new DomainValidationException("file.id", "文件名与内嵌项目标识不一致，请通过导入项目另存");
0071              }
0072
0073              return new StoredProject(decoded.Project, Hash(bytes), file);
0074          }
0075          finally
0076          {
0077              gate.Release();
0078          }
0079      }
0080
0081      public async Task<StoredProject> LoadBackupAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken cancellation = default)
0082      {
0083          await gate.WaitAsync(cancellation);
0084          try
0085          {
0086              var file = PathFor(id);
0087              var backup = file + ".bak";
0088              var bytes = await ReadBoundedAsync(backup, cancellation);
0089              var decoded = codec.Decode(bytes);
0090              if (decoded.Project.Id != id)
0091              {
0092                  throw new DomainValidationException("backup.id", "备份项目标识与文件名不一致");
0093              }
0094
0095              var current = File.Exists(file) ? Hash(await ReadBoundedAsync(file, cancellation)) : "";
0096              return new StoredProject(decoded.Project, current, backup, true);
0097          }
0098          finally
0099          {
0100              gate.Release();
0101          }
0102      }
0103
0104      public async Task<SaveReceipt> SaveAsync(AcousticProject project, string? expectedETag, CancellationToken cancellation = default)
0105      {
0106          var bytes = codec.Encode(project);
0107          if (bytes.Length > Software.MaximumFileBytes)
0108          {
0109              throw new DomainValidationException("file", "项目超过12MiB，请减少记录或分项目保存");
0110          }
0111
0112          await gate.WaitAsync(cancellation);
0113          string? temporary = null;
0114          try
0115          {
0116              var file = PathFor(project.Id);
0117              // Share.None coordinates cooperating application instances before the
0118              // ETag check. The persistent lock file avoids unlink/recreate races.
0119              using var fileLock = new FileStream(file + ".lock", FileMode.OpenOrCreate,
0120                  FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.None);
0121              var exists = File.Exists(file);
0122              var actual = exists ? Hash(await ReadBoundedAsync(file, cancellation)) : null;
0123              var expected = string.IsNullOrEmpty(expectedETag) ? null : expectedETag;
0124              if (actual != expected)
0125              {
0126                  throw new ProjectConflictException();
0127              }
0128
0129              temporary = file + "." + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".tmp";
0130              await using (var stream = new FileStream(temporary, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None,
0131                  81920, FileOptions.Asynchronous | FileOptions.WriteThrough))
0132              {
0133                  await stream.WriteAsync(bytes, cancellation);
0134                  await stream.FlushAsync(cancellation);
0135                  stream.Flush(flushToDisk: true);
0136              }
0137
0138              if (exists)
0139              {
0140                  File.Copy(file, file + ".bak", overwrite: true);
0141              }
0142
0143              File.Move(temporary, file, overwrite: true);
0144              temporary = null;
0145              return new SaveReceipt(file, exists ? file + ".bak" : "", Hash(bytes), bytes.Length);
0146          }
0147          finally
0148          {
0149              if (temporary is not null && File.Exists(temporary))
0150              {
0151                  File.Delete(temporary);
0152              }
0153
0154              gate.Release();
0155          }
0156      }
0157
0158      public string PathFor(Guid id)
0159      {
0160          if (id == Guid.Empty)
0161          {
0162              throw new DomainValidationException("project.id", "项目标识不能为空");
0163          }
0164
0165          return System.IO.Path.Combine(directory, id.ToString("N") + Software.Extension);
0166      }
0167
0168      private static async Task<byte[]> ReadBoundedAsync(string file, CancellationToken cancellation)
0169      {
0170          var info = new FileInfo(file);
0171          if (info.Length > Software.MaximumFileBytes)
0172          {
0173              throw new DomainValidationException("file", "文件超过12MiB，拒绝读取");
0174          }
0175
0176          return await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(file, cancellation);
0177      }
0178
0179      private static string Hash(byte[] bytes)
0180      {
0181          return Convert.ToHexStringLower(SHA256.HashData(bytes));
0182      }
0183  }

src/AcousticSeal.Core/IO/ResultExport.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Globalization;
0002  using System.Net;
0003  using System.Text;
0004  using System.Text.Json;
0005  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0006  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0007
0008  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0009
0010  public sealed record ExportDocument(string Filename, string ContentType, byte[] Bytes);
0011
0012  public sealed class ResultExport
0013  {
0014      public ExportDocument Project(AcousticProject project)
0015      {
0016          return new ExportDocument(SafeName(project.Name) + Software.Extension,
0017              "application/json; charset=utf-8", new ProjectCodec().Encode(project));
0018      }
0019
0020      public ExportDocument Measurements(AcousticProject project)
0021      {
0022          var rows = new List<IEnumerable<object?>>
0023          {
0024              new object?[] { "trial_id", "frequency_hz", "level_db" }
0025          };
0026          foreach (var trial in project.Trials)
0027          {
0028              foreach (var band in trial.Bands.OrderBy(band => band.Frequency))
0029              {
0030                  rows.Add(new object?[] { trial.Id, band.Frequency, band.Level });
0031              }
0032          }
0033
0034          return Csv(project, ".measurements.csv", rows);
0035      }
0036
0037      public ExportDocument Results(AcousticProject project, AnalysisReport report)
0038      {
0039          var rows = new List<IEnumerable<object?>>
0040          {
0041              new object?[]
0042              {
0043                  "project_id", "project", "revision", "software_version", "schema_version",
0044                  "pair_id", "purpose", "baseline_id", "target_id", "baseline", "target",
0045                  "source_kind", "baseline_source", "target_source", "baseline_condition_id",
0046                  "target_condition_id", "frequency_hz", "baseline_db", "target_db",
0047                  "measured_baseline_minus_target_db", "status", "blocking_reasons", "warnings",
0048                  "level_reference", "settings_json", "input_hash"
0049              }
0050          };
0051          var trials = project.Trials.ToDictionary(trial => trial.Id);
0052          foreach (var pair in report.Pairs)
0053          {
0054              var bands = pair.Bands.Count > 0 ? pair.Bands : [new BandDifference { Status = pair.Status }];
0055              foreach (var band in bands)
0056              {
0057                  rows.Add(new object?[]
0058                  {
0059                      project.Id, project.Name, project.Revision, Software.Version, project.SchemaVersion,
0060                      pair.PairId, pair.Purpose, pair.BaselineId, pair.TargetId, pair.BaselineName, pair.TargetName,
0061                      pair.Source, pair.BaselineSource, pair.TargetSource,
0062                      trials[pair.BaselineId].ConditionId, trials[pair.TargetId].ConditionId,
0063                      band.Frequency == 0 ? null : band.Frequency, band.Baseline, band.Target, band.Difference,
0064                      band.Status, string.Join("；", pair.BlockingReasons), string.Join("；", pair.Warnings),
0065                      Software.LevelUnit, JsonSerializer.Serialize(project.Settings, ProjectCodec.Options),
0066                      ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project)
0067                  });
0068              }
0069          }
0070
0071          return Csv(project, ".results.csv", rows);
0072      }
0073
0074      public ExportDocument AnalysisJson(AcousticProject project, AnalysisReport report)
0075      {
0076          var content = new
0077          {
0078              softwareName = Software.Name,
0079              softwareVersion = Software.Version,
0080              unit = Software.LevelUnit,
0081              differenceDefinition = "baseline-target",
0082              inputHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project),
0083              limitation = Software.Limitation,
0084              project,
0085              report
0086          };
0087          return new ExportDocument(SafeName(project.Name) + ".analysis.json", "application/json; charset=utf-8",
0088              JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(content, ProjectCodec.Options));
0089      }
0090
0091      public ExportDocument Report(AcousticProject project, AnalysisReport report)
0092      {
0093          return new ExportDocument(SafeName(project.Name) + ".report.html", "text/html; charset=utf-8",
0094              Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(new HtmlReport().Build(project, report)));
0095      }
0096
0097      public static string SafeName(string value)
0098      {
0099          var invalid = "<>:\"/\\|?*";
0100          var name = new string(value.Select(character => char.IsControl(character) || invalid.Contains(character) ? '_' : character).ToArray());
0101          name = name.Trim().TrimEnd('.');
0102          return string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) ? "声学项目" : name[..Math.Min(name.Length, 100)];
0103      }
0104
0105      private static ExportDocument Csv(AcousticProject project, string extension, IEnumerable<IEnumerable<object?>> rows)
0106      {
0107          var builder = new StringBuilder("\uFEFF");
0108          foreach (var row in rows)
0109          {
0110              builder.AppendLine(string.Join(",", row.Select(Cell)));
0111          }
0112
0113          return new ExportDocument(SafeName(project.Name) + extension, "text/csv; charset=utf-8", Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(builder.ToString()));
0114      }
0115
0116      private static string Cell(object? value)
0117      {
0118          if (value is null)
0119          {
0120              return "";
0121          }
0122
0123          var text = value is IFormattable formatted
0124              ? formatted.ToString(null, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
0125              : value.ToString() ?? "";
0126          if (value is string && text.Length > 0 && "=+-@\t\r".Contains(text[0]))
0127          {
0128              text = "'" + text;
0129          }
0130
0131          return "\"" + text.Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
0132      }
0133  }
0134

0165              project.Locations.Select(location => new object?[]

0200                  ["封堵位置", project.LocationNames(trial.LocationIds)],
0201                  ["重复组", trial.RepeatGroup],

0209                  .Select(band => new object?[] { band.Frequency, N(band.Level), band.Note }));
0210          }

0218              {

0221

0223              {
0224                  Notice(string.Join("；", pair.Warnings));

0230              {

0244          foreach (var effect in report.Effects)

0261              Table(["Hz", "n", "缺失", "算术均值dB", "能量均值dB", "样本SD dB", "极差dB", "状态"], group.Bands.Select(band => new object?[]
0262              {

0264                  N(band.Statistics.EnergyMean), N(band.Statistics.StandardDeviation), N(band.Statistics.Range), band.Status.Text()
0265              }));

0277          {

0282          {

0287          {
0288              Software.Time(entry.Time), entry.Revision, entry.Action, entry.Entity, entry.Detail, entry.InputHash
0289          }));
0290          Heading("12 方法与边界");
0291          Paragraph("ΔL=L基线−L目标。等权能量均值=10log10(Σ10^(Li/10)/n)；不重叠频带合成去除分母n。只对共同有效配对比较；频带合成须集合一致、无缺失且用户确认互不重叠。");

0293          Notice(Software.Limitation);
0294          output.AppendLine("</body></html>");
0295          return output.ToString();
