0298      private void Coverage(AnalysisReport report)
0299      {
0300          Heading("8 试验覆盖和时间线");
0301          Paragraph(report.Coverage.Note);
0302          Table(["试验类型", "记录数"], report.Coverage.TrialTypes.Select(item => new object?[] { item.Key.Text(), item.Value }));
0303          Table(["工况", "试验数", "缺失口径"], report.Coverage.Conditions.Select(item => new object?[] { item.Name, item.TrialCount, string.Join("；", item.Missing) }));
0304          Table(["封堵位置", "单项数", "组合数", "有效配对数"], report.Coverage.Locations.Select(item => new object?[]
0305          {
0306              item.Name, item.SingleCount, item.CombinationCount, item.ValidPairCount
0307          }));
0308          Table(["Hz", "有效", "明确缺失", "未声明"], report.Coverage.Frequencies.Select(item => new object?[]
0309          {
0310              item.Frequency, item.Valid, item.Missing, item.Undeclared
0311          }));
0312          Table(["时间", "试验", "类型", "来源", "有效频带", "目标配对数"], report.Coverage.Timeline.Select(item => new object?[]
0313          {
0314              Software.Time(item.Time), item.Name, item.Kind.Text(), item.Source.Text(), item.ValidBands, item.TargetPairs
0315          }));
0316      }
0317
0318      private void Matrix(AcousticProject project, AnalysisReport report)
0319      {
0320          Heading("9 单项与组合测得差值矩阵");
0321          Paragraph("各列使用自己的显式基线、工况和来源；不能直接作独立贡献排名或单项相加预测。");
0322          var pairs = report.Pairs.Where(pair => pair.Purpose == PairPurpose.Effect).ToList();
0323          var columns = new List<string> { "Hz" };
0324          columns.AddRange(pairs.Select(pair => pair.TargetName + "；基线=" + pair.BaselineName + "；" + pair.Source.Text()));
0325          var rows = new List<object?[]>();
0326          foreach (var frequency in pairs.SelectMany(pair => pair.Bands.Select(band => band.Frequency)).Distinct().Order())
0327          {
0328              var cells = new List<object?> { frequency };
0329              foreach (var pair in pairs)
0330              {
0331                  var band = pair.Bands.FirstOrDefault(band => band.Frequency == frequency);
0332                  cells.Add(band?.Difference is { } value ? N(value) : band?.Status.Text() ?? "未声明");
0333              }
0334
0335              rows.Add(cells.ToArray());
0336          }
0337
0338          Table(columns, rows);
0339      }
0340
0341      public static string Chart(PairResult pair)
0342      {
0343          var bands = pair.Bands.Where(band => band.Difference.HasValue).ToList();
0344          if (bands.Count == 0)
0345          {
0346              return "<p>无可绘制差值，未生成图表。</p>";
0347          }
0348
0349          if (bands.Count > 30)
0350          {
0351              return "<p>频带超过30个，为保证文字可读，报告使用完整数值表；界面可扫描较窄频率范围查看图表。</p>";
0352          }
0353
0354          var maximum = Math.Max(1, bands.Max(band => Math.Abs(band.Difference!.Value)));
0355          var svg = new StringBuilder("<svg viewBox=\"0 0 820 270\" role=\"img\" aria-label=\"测得声级差图，横轴Hz，纵轴dB\"><text x=\"8\" y=\"20\" font-size=\"12\">ΔL / dB</text><line x1=\"60\" y1=\"130\" x2=\"800\" y2=\"130\" stroke=\"#a5bac8\"/>");
0356          svg.Append("<text x=\"8\" y=\"45\" font-size=\"11\">").Append(N(maximum)).Append("</text><text x=\"20\" y=\"135\" font-size=\"11\">0</text>");
0357          for (var index = 0; index < bands.Count; index++)
0358          {
0359              var band = bands[index];
0360              var x = 60 + (index + 0.5) * 740 / bands.Count;
0361              var width = Math.Min(45, 500.0 / bands.Count);
0362              var height = Math.Abs(band.Difference!.Value) / maximum * 85;
0363              var y = band.Difference >= 0 ? 130 - height : 130;
0364              svg.Append("<rect x=\"").Append(C(x - width / 2)).Append("\" y=\"").Append(C(y)).Append("\" width=\"").Append(C(width))
0365                  .Append("\" height=\"").Append(C(height)).Append("\" fill=\"").Append(band.Difference >= 0 ? "#148c86" : "#be792e").Append("\"/>");
0366              svg.Append("<text x=\"").Append(C(x)).Append("\" y=\"245\" text-anchor=\"middle\" font-size=\"10\">").Append(C(band.Frequency)).Append("</text>");
0367              svg.Append("<text x=\"").Append(C(x)).Append("\" y=\"").Append(C(band.Difference >= 0 ? y - 8 : y + height + 15))
0368                  .Append("\" text-anchor=\"middle\" font-size=\"11\">").Append(N(band.Difference)).Append("</text>");
0369          }
0370
0371          return svg.Append("<text x=\"775\" y=\"265\" font-size=\"12\">Hz</text></svg>").ToString();
0372      }
0373
0374      private void Heading(string text)
0375      {
0376          output.Append("<h2>").Append(E(text)).AppendLine("</h2>");
0377      }
0378
0379      private void Subheading(string text)
0380      {
0381          output.Append("<h3>").Append(E(text)).AppendLine("</h3>");
0382      }
0383
0384      private void Paragraph(string text)
0385      {
0386          output.Append("<p>").Append(E(text)).AppendLine("</p>");
0387      }
0388
0389      private void Notice(string text)
0390      {
0391          output.Append("<p class=\"notice\">").Append(E(text)).AppendLine("</p>");
0392      }
0393
0394      private void Table(IEnumerable<string> headings, IEnumerable<object?[]> rows)
0395      {
0396          var columns = headings.ToList();
0397          output.Append("<table><thead><tr>");
0398          foreach (var heading in columns)
0399          {
0400              output.Append("<th>").Append(E(heading)).Append("</th>");
0401          }
0402
0403          output.Append("</tr></thead><tbody>");
0404          var count = 0;
0405          foreach (var row in rows)
0406          {
0407              output.Append("<tr>");
0408              foreach (var cell in row)
0409              {
0410                  output.Append("<td>").Append(E(cell?.ToString() ?? "—")).Append("</td>");
0411              }
0412
0413              output.Append("</tr>");
0414              count++;
0415          }
0416
0417          if (count == 0)
0418          {
0419              output.Append("<tr><td colspan=\"").Append(columns.Count).Append("\">暂无记录</td></tr>");
0420          }
0421
0422          output.AppendLine("</tbody></table>");
0423      }
0424
0425      private static string E(string value)
0426      {
0427          return WebUtility.HtmlEncode(value);
0428      }
0429
0430      private static string N(double? value)
0431      {
0432          return Software.Format(value, 6);
0433      }
0434
0435      private static string C(double value)
0436      {
0437          return value.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
0438      }
0439  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Operations/BatchPairing.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0004
0005  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Operations;
0006
0007  public sealed record ProposedPair(TrialPair Pair, string Baseline, string Target, List<string> Reasons, bool Duplicate);
0008  public sealed record PairingPreview(string ProjectHash, List<ProposedPair> Rows)
0009  {
0010      public bool CanApply => Rows.Count > 0 && Rows.All(row => !row.Duplicate);
0011  }
0012
0013  public sealed class BatchPairing
0014  {
0015      public PairingPreview Preview(AcousticProject project, Guid baselineId, IReadOnlyList<Guid> targetIds, PairPurpose purpose)
0016      {
0017          var baseline = project.Trials.FirstOrDefault(trial => trial.Id == baselineId && trial.Kind == TrialKind.Baseline)
0018              ?? throw new DomainValidationException("baseline", "先明确选择一个基线试验");
0019          if (targetIds.Count == 0 || targetIds.Distinct().Count() != targetIds.Count)
0020          {
0021              throw new DomainValidationException("targets", "目标列表不能为空或重复");
0022          }
0023
0024          var rows = new List<ProposedPair>();
0025          var validator = new ProjectValidator();
0026          var comparator = new Comparability();
0027          foreach (var id in targetIds)
0028          {
0029              var target = project.Trials.FirstOrDefault(trial => trial.Id == id)
0030                  ?? throw new DomainValidationException("target", "目标试验不存在");
0031              var pair = new TrialPair
0032              {
0033                  BaselineId = baseline.Id,
0034                  TargetId = id,
0035                  Purpose = purpose,
0036                  Notes = "用户显式勾选目标并确认批量配对；共享基线时不作为独立配对估计区间"
0037              };
0038              var duplicate = project.Pairs.Any(item => item.BaselineId == pair.BaselineId && item.TargetId == pair.TargetId && item.Purpose == pair.Purpose);
0039              var candidate = ProjectCodec.Clone(project);
0040              candidate.Pairs.Add(pair);
0041              var structural = validator.Inspect(candidate).Where(error => error.Path.StartsWith("pairs", StringComparison.Ordinal)).ToList();
0042              if (structural.Count > 0 && !duplicate)
0043              {
0044                  throw new DomainValidationException(structural);
0045              }
0046
0047              var left = project.Conditions.Single(condition => condition.Id == baseline.ConditionId);
0048              var right = project.Conditions.Single(condition => condition.Id == target.ConditionId);
0049              var reasons = comparator.Inspect(left, right, project.Settings);
0050              if (baseline.Source != target.Source)
0051              {
0052                  reasons.Add("来源类别不一致");
0053              }
0054
0055              rows.Add(new ProposedPair(pair, baseline.Name, target.Name, reasons, duplicate));
0056          }
0057
0058          return new PairingPreview(ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project), rows);
0059      }
0060
0061      public void Apply(ProjectSession session, PairingPreview preview)
0062      {
0063          if (!preview.CanApply || ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(session.Project) != preview.ProjectHash)
0064          {
0065              throw new DomainValidationException("pairing", "预检重复、为空或已过期，请重新选择目标预检");
0066          }
0067
0068          session.Apply("用户确认批量配对", session.Project.Id.ToString(), project =>
0069              project.Pairs.AddRange(preview.Rows.Select(row => ProjectCodec.Clone(row.Pair))));
0070      }
0071  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Operations/ProjectCommands.cs
================================================================================
0001  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0003
0004  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Operations;
0005
0006  public static class ProjectCommands
0007  {
0008      public static void SaveLocation(ProjectSession session, SealLocation location)
0009      {
0010          session.Apply("应用封堵位置", location.Id.ToString(), project => Upsert(project.Locations, location, item => item.Id));
0011      }
0012
0013      public static void SaveCondition(ProjectSession session, MeasurementCondition condition)
0014      {
0015          session.Apply("应用工况", condition.Id.ToString(), project => Upsert(project.Conditions, condition, item => item.Id));
0016      }
0017
0018      public static void SaveTrial(ProjectSession session, AcousticTrial trial)
0019      {
0020          session.Apply("应用试验", trial.Id.ToString(), project => Upsert(project.Trials, trial, item => item.Id));
0021      }
0022
0023      public static void SavePair(ProjectSession session, TrialPair pair)
0024      {
0025          session.Apply("应用显式配对", pair.Id.ToString(), project => Upsert(project.Pairs, pair, item => item.Id));
0026      }
0027
0028      public static void DeleteLocation(ProjectSession session, Guid id)
0029      {
0030          session.Apply("删除封堵位置", id.ToString(), project =>
0031          {
0032              var related = project.Trials.Where(trial => trial.LocationIds.Contains(id)).Select(trial => trial.Name).ToList();
0033              EnsureNoReferences(related);
0034              EnsureRemoved(project.Locations.RemoveAll(item => item.Id == id));
0035          });
0036      }
0037
0038      public static void DeleteCondition(ProjectSession session, Guid id)
0039      {
0040          session.Apply("删除工况", id.ToString(), project =>
0041          {
0042              var related = project.Trials.Where(trial => trial.ConditionId == id).Select(trial => trial.Name).ToList();
0043              EnsureNoReferences(related);
0044              EnsureRemoved(project.Conditions.RemoveAll(item => item.Id == id));
0045          });
0046      }
0047
0048      public static void DeleteTrial(ProjectSession session, Guid id)
0049      {
0050          session.Apply("删除试验", id.ToString(), project =>
0051          {
0052              var related = project.Pairs.Where(pair => pair.BaselineId == id || pair.TargetId == id)
0053                  .Select(pair => pair.Id.ToString()).ToList();
0054              EnsureNoReferences(related);
0055              EnsureRemoved(project.Trials.RemoveAll(item => item.Id == id));
0056          });
0057      }
0058
0059      public static void DeletePair(ProjectSession session, Guid id)
0060      {
0061          session.Apply("删除配对", id.ToString(), project => EnsureRemoved(project.Pairs.RemoveAll(item => item.Id == id)));
0062      }
0063
0064      public static Guid DuplicateTrial(ProjectSession session, Guid id)
0065      {
0066          var newId = Guid.NewGuid();
0067          session.Apply("复制试验结构", id.ToString(), project =>
0068          {
0069              var original = project.Trials.FirstOrDefault(trial => trial.Id == id)
0070                  ?? throw new DomainValidationException("trial", "试验不存在");
0071              var copy = ProjectCodec.Clone(original);
0072              copy.Id = newId;
0073              copy.Name = (copy.Name.Length > 140 ? copy.Name[..140] : copy.Name) + " 副本（待录入）";
0074              copy.Bands = [];
0075              copy.RepeatGroup = "";
0076              copy.Timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
0077              copy.Notes = "复制结构未复制测量与配对，时间和来源须复核。";
0078              project.Trials.Add(copy);
0079          });
0080          return newId;
0081      }
0082
0083      public static void SetArchived(ProjectSession session, bool archived)
0084      {
0085          session.Apply(archived ? "归档项目" : "解除归档", session.Project.Id.ToString(),
0086              project => project.Status = archived ? ProjectStatus.Archived : ProjectStatus.Active, allowArchived: true);
0087      }
0088
0089      public static void SaveSettings(ProjectSession session, AnalysisSettings settings)
0090      {
0091          session.Apply("应用参数并重新计算全部历史输入", session.Project.Id.ToString(),
0092              project => project.Settings = ProjectCodec.Clone(settings));
0093      }
0094
0095      private static void Upsert<T>(List<T> items, T value, Func<T, Guid> key)
0096      {
0097          var index = items.FindIndex(item => key(item) == key(value));
0098          if (index < 0)
0099          {
0100              items.Add(ProjectCodec.Clone(value));
0101          }
0102          else
0103          {
0104              items[index] = ProjectCodec.Clone(value);
0105          }
0106      }
0107
0108      private static void EnsureNoReferences(List<string> related)
0109      {
0110          if (related.Count > 0)
0111          {
0112              throw new DomainValidationException("references", "仍被引用，请先处理关联对象：" + string.Join("、", related.Take(10)));
0113          }
0114      }
0115
0116      private static void EnsureRemoved(int count)
0117      {
0118          if (count == 0)
0119          {
0120              throw new DomainValidationException("id", "对象已不存在，请重新选择");
0121          }
0122      }
0123  }

================================================================================
src/AcousticSeal.Core/Operations/ProjectSession.cs
================================================================================
0001  using System.Text.Json;
0002  using AcousticSeal.Core.Analysis;
0003  using AcousticSeal.Core.Domain;
0004  using AcousticSeal.Core.IO;
0005
0006  namespace AcousticSeal.Core.Operations;
0007
0008  public sealed class ProjectSession
0009  {
0010      private readonly ProjectValidator validator = new();
0011      private readonly AnalysisEngine engine = new();
0012      private readonly List<HistoryFrame> undo = [];
0013      private readonly List<HistoryFrame> redo = [];
0014      public AcousticProject Project { get; private set; } = AcousticProject.Empty();
0015      public AnalysisReport Analysis { get; private set; }
0016      public bool Dirty { get; private set; }
0017      public string? SavedETag { get; private set; }
0018      public string SavedPath { get; private set; } = "";
0019      public bool CanUndo => undo.Count > 0;
0020      public bool CanRedo => redo.Count > 0;
0021      public string UndoLabel => undo.Count > 0 ? undo[^1].Action : "";
0022      public string RedoLabel => redo.Count > 0 ? redo[^1].Action : "";
0023
0024      public ProjectSession()
0025      {
0026          Analysis = engine.Calculate(Project);
0027      }
0028
0029      public void Open(AcousticProject project, string? etag = null, string path = "", bool changed = false)
0030      {
0031          validator.Ensure(project);
0032          var copy = ProjectCodec.Clone(project);
0033          var analysis = engine.Calculate(copy);
0034          Project = copy;
0035          Analysis = analysis;
0036          Dirty = changed;
0037          SavedETag = etag;
0038          SavedPath = path;
0039          undo.Clear();
0040          redo.Clear();
0041      }
0042
0043      public void MarkSaved(SaveReceipt receipt)
0044      {
0045          SavedETag = receipt.ETag;
0046          SavedPath = receipt.Path;
0047          Dirty = false;
0048      }
0049
0050      public void Apply(string action, string entity, Action<AcousticProject> change, bool allowArchived = false)
0051      {
0052          if (Project.Status == ProjectStatus.Archived && !allowArchived)
0053          {
0054              throw new DomainValidationException("status", "项目已归档，请先解除归档或创建副本");
0055          }
0056
0057          var before = ProjectCodec.Clone(Project);
0058          var candidate = ProjectCodec.Clone(Project);
0059          change(candidate);
0060          var detail = DescribeChanges(before, candidate);
0061          Stamp(candidate, action, entity, detail);
0062          validator.Ensure(candidate);
0063          var analysis = engine.Calculate(candidate);
0064          undo.Add(new HistoryFrame(before, ProjectCodec.Clone(candidate), action));
0065          if (undo.Count > 20)
0066          {
0067              undo.RemoveAt(0);
0068          }
0069
0070          redo.Clear();
0071          Project = candidate;
0072          Analysis = analysis;
0073          Dirty = true;
0074      }
0075
0076      public void Undo()
0077      {
0078          if (!CanUndo)
0079          {
0080              return;
0081          }
0082
0083          var frame = undo[^1];
0084          RestoreHistory(frame.Before, "撤销：" + frame.Action);
0085          undo.RemoveAt(undo.Count - 1);
0086          redo.Add(frame);
0087      }
0088
0089      public void Redo()
0090      {
0091          if (!CanRedo)
0092          {
0093              return;
0094          }
0095
0096          var frame = redo[^1];
0097          RestoreHistory(frame.After, "重做：" + frame.Action);
0098          redo.RemoveAt(redo.Count - 1);
0099          undo.Add(frame);
0100      }
0101
0102      public void MakeCopy()
0103      {
0104          var copy = ProjectCodec.Clone(Project);
0105          var original = copy.Id;
0106          copy.Id = Guid.NewGuid();
0107          copy.Name = (copy.Name.Length > 150 ? copy.Name[..150] : copy.Name) + " 副本";
0108          copy.Status = ProjectStatus.Active;
0109          Stamp(copy, "复制项目", copy.Id.ToString(), "来源项目=" + original + "；测量来源仍沿用原记录，不代表重新采集");
0110          Open(copy, changed: true);
0111      }
0112
0113      public void RecordReview(string reviewer, string comment)
0114      {
0115          var snapshot = Analysis;
0116          var input = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(Project);
0117          Apply("记录分析复核", Project.Id.ToString(), project =>
0118          {
0119              project.Reviews.Add(new ReviewRecord
0120              {
0121                  InputRevision = project.Revision,
0122                  Reviewer = reviewer.Trim(),
0123                  Comment = comment.Trim(),
0124                  ValidPairs = snapshot.ValidPairCount,
0125                  BlockedPairs = snapshot.BlockedPairCount,
0126                  BlockingReasons = snapshot.Pairs.SelectMany(pair => pair.BlockingReasons.Select(reason => pair.Title + "：" + reason)).ToList(),
0127                  SettingsJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(project.Settings, ProjectCodec.Options),
0128                  InputHash = input
0129              });
0130          });
0131      }
0132
0133      private void RestoreHistory(AcousticProject state, string action)
0134      {
0135          var candidate = ProjectCodec.Clone(state);
0136          candidate.Audit = ProjectCodec.Clone(Project.Audit);
0137          candidate.Reviews = ProjectCodec.Clone(Project.Reviews);
0138          candidate.Revision = Project.Revision;
0139          Stamp(candidate, action, candidate.Id.ToString(), "恢复数据状态，保留累计审计与复核；撤销记录仅限当前会话20步");
0140          validator.Ensure(candidate);
0141          var analysis = engine.Calculate(candidate);
0142          Project = candidate;
0143          Analysis = analysis;
0144          Dirty = true;
0145      }
0146
0147      private static void Stamp(AcousticProject project, string action, string entity, string detail)
0148      {
0149          project.Revision++;
0150          project.UpdatedAt = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
0151          project.SoftwareVersion = Software.Version;
0152          project.Audit.Add(new AuditEntry
0153          {
0154              Revision = project.Revision,
0155              Action = action,
0156              Entity = entity,
0157              Detail = detail.Length > 16000 ? detail[..16000] : detail,
0158              InputHash = ProjectCodec.Fingerprint(project)
0159          });
0160          if (project.Audit.Count > 10000)
0161          {
0162              project.Audit.RemoveRange(0, project.Audit.Count - 10000);
0163          }
0164      }
0165
0166      private static string DescribeChanges(AcousticProject before, AcousticProject after)
0167      {
0168          using var left = JsonDocument.Parse(JsonSerializer.Serialize(before, ProjectCodec.Options));
0169          using var right = JsonDocument.Parse(JsonSerializer.Serialize(after, ProjectCodec.Options));
0170          var changes = new List<string>();
0171          foreach (var property in right.RootElement.EnumerateObject())
0172          {
0173              if (property.Name is "audit" or "reviews")
0174              {
0175                  continue;
0176              }
0177
0178              if (!left.RootElement.TryGetProperty(property.Name, out var previous) || previous.GetRawText() != property.Value.GetRawText())
0179              {
0180                  changes.Add(property.Name);
0181              }
0182          }
0183
0184          return changes.Count == 0 ? "记录复核或状态操作" : "修改字段/集合：" + string.Join("、", changes);
0185      }
0186
0187      private sealed record HistoryFrame(AcousticProject Before, AcousticProject After, string Action);
0188  }
